pcall(function()
    if not game:IsLoaded() then
        game.Loaded:Wait()
    end
end)

-- Master Shared Namespace Table (Prevents Luau 200-Local & 60-Upvalue Limit Errors)
local Ony = {}

-- ============================================================================
-- SECTION 1: CORE SERVICES, LOCALSCRIPT SANDBOX & ENVIRONMENT BOOTSTRAPPER
-- ============================================================================
local function GetSafeService(serviceName)
    local ok, svc = pcall(function()
        return game:GetService(serviceName)
    end)
    if ok and svc then
        return svc
    end
    return nil
end
Ony.GetSafeService = GetSafeService

local Players = GetSafeService("Players")
local RunService = GetSafeService("RunService")
local TweenService = GetSafeService("TweenService")
local UserInputService = GetSafeService("UserInputService")
local ReplicatedStorage = GetSafeService("ReplicatedStorage")
local TeleportService = GetSafeService("TeleportService")
local HttpService = GetSafeService("HttpService")
local TextChatService = GetSafeService("TextChatService")
local VirtualUser = GetSafeService("VirtualUser")
local Workspace = GetSafeService("Workspace") or workspace
local Lighting = GetSafeService("Lighting")
local StatsService = GetSafeService("Stats")
local SoundService = GetSafeService("SoundService")

local LocalPlayer = Players and Players.LocalPlayer or nil
local waitAttempts = 0
while not LocalPlayer and waitAttempts < 100 do
    waitAttempts = waitAttempts + 1
    task.wait(0.05)
    LocalPlayer = Players and Players.LocalPlayer or nil
end

local Camera = Workspace and Workspace.CurrentCamera or nil
if Workspace then
    pcall(function()
        Workspace:GetPropertyChangedSignal("CurrentCamera"):Connect(function()
            if Workspace.CurrentCamera then
                Camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
            end
        end)
    end)
end

-- // Executor & LocalScript Capability Detection Matrix
local ExecutorCaps = {
    HasDrawing = false,
    HasGetHui = (type(gethui) == "function"),
    HasFireTouchInterest = (type(firetouchinterest) == "function"),
    HasFireProximityPrompt = (type(fireproximityprompt) == "function"),
    HasHookMetaMethod = (type(hookmetamethod) == "function"),
    HasGetRawMetatable = (type(getrawmetatable) == "function"),
    HasSetReadOnly = (type(setreadonly) == "function"),
    HasFileSystem = (
        type(writefile) == "function"
        and type(readfile) == "function"
        and type(isfile) == "function"
        and type(makefolder) == "function"
        and type(isfolder) == "function"
    ),
    HasHttpRequest = (
        type(request) == "function"
        or type(http_request) == "function"
        or (type(syn) == "table" and type(syn.request) == "function")
        or (type(http) == "table" and type(http.request) == "function")
    ),
}
Ony.ExecutorCaps = ExecutorCaps

ExecutorCaps.HasDrawing = pcall(function()
    if (type(Drawing) ~= "table" and type(Drawing) ~= "userdata") or type(Drawing.new) ~= "function" then
        error("Drawing API unavailable")
    end
    local testLine = Drawing.new("Line")
    local testCircle = Drawing.new("Circle")
    local testSquare = Drawing.new("Square")
    local testTriangle = Drawing.new("Triangle")
    testLine:Remove()
    testCircle:Remove()
    testSquare:Remove()
    testTriangle:Remove()
end)

local function SafeHttpRequest(options)
    local reqFunc = (type(request) == "function" and request)
        or (type(http_request) == "function" and http_request)
        or (type(syn) == "table" and type(syn.request) == "function" and syn.request)
        or (type(http) == "table" and type(http.request) == "function" and http.request)
    if reqFunc then
        local ok, res = pcall(reqFunc, options)
        if ok then
            return res
        end
    end
    if options and options.Method == "GET" and options.Url then
        local ok, body = pcall(function()
            return game:HttpGet(options.Url)
        end)
        if ok and body then
            return { Body = body, StatusCode = 200 }
        end
    end
    return nil
end
Ony.SafeHttpRequest = SafeHttpRequest

-- // LocalScript & Executor Safe ScreenGui Parenting
-- Prioritizes LocalPlayer.PlayerGui so it is 100% guaranteed to render inside LocalScripts & all Executors!
local GUI_NAME = "OnyWtf_MM2_Suite"
Ony.GUI_NAME = GUI_NAME

local function ParentGuiSafely(guiInstance)
    local parented = false

    -- 1. Try LocalPlayer.PlayerGui first (100% compatible with Roblox LocalScripts and all Executors)
    if LocalPlayer then
        parented = pcall(function()
            local playerGui = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
                or LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui", 5)
            if not playerGui then
                error("PlayerGui not ready")
            end
            if playerGui:FindFirstChild(GUI_NAME) then
                playerGui[GUI_NAME]:Destroy()
            end
            guiInstance.Parent = playerGui
            if guiInstance.Parent ~= playerGui then
                error("Parent assignment failed")
            end
        end)
    end

    -- 2. Fallback to gethui() if PlayerGui failed
    if not parented and type(gethui) == "function" then
        parented = pcall(function()
            local target = gethui()
            if target:FindFirstChild(GUI_NAME) then
                target[GUI_NAME]:Destroy()
            end
            guiInstance.Parent = target
        end)
    end

    -- 3. Fallback to CoreGui if both above failed
    if not parented then
        pcall(function()
            local coreGui = game:GetService("CoreGui")
            if coreGui:FindFirstChild(GUI_NAME) then
                coreGui[GUI_NAME]:Destroy()
            end
            guiInstance.Parent = coreGui
        end)
    end
end
Ony.ParentGuiSafely = ParentGuiSafely

-- // Cleanup Previous Instance & Connections if Re-Executed
local GlobalEnv = (type(getgenv) == "function" and getgenv()) or _G
if type(GlobalEnv) ~= "table" then
    GlobalEnv = {}
end
Ony.GlobalEnv = GlobalEnv

if GlobalEnv.OnyWtfCleanup and type(GlobalEnv.OnyWtfCleanup) == "function" then
    pcall(GlobalEnv.OnyWtfCleanup)
end

Ony.ActiveConnections = {}
Ony.ActiveDrawingObjects = {}
Ony.CreatedWorldInstances = {}

local function TrackConnection(conn)
    if conn then
        table.insert(Ony.ActiveConnections, conn)
    end
    return conn
end
Ony.TrackConnection = TrackConnection

local function TrackWorldInstance(inst)
    if inst then
        table.insert(Ony.CreatedWorldInstances, inst)
    end
    return inst
end
Ony.TrackWorldInstance = TrackWorldInstance

local function CreateSafeDrawing(drawingType, props)
    if not ExecutorCaps.HasDrawing then
        return nil
    end
    local ok, obj = pcall(function()
        return Drawing.new(drawingType)
    end)
    if not ok or not obj then
        return nil
    end
    if type(props) == "table" then
        for k, v in pairs(props) do
            pcall(function()
                obj[k] = v
            end)
        end
    end
    table.insert(Ony.ActiveDrawingObjects, obj)
    return obj
end
Ony.CreateSafeDrawing = CreateSafeDrawing

    Ambient = Lighting and Lighting.Ambient or Color3.fromRGB(127, 127, 127),
    OutdoorAmbient = Lighting and Lighting.OutdoorAmbient or Color3.fromRGB(127, 127, 127),
}

    FieldOfView = Camera and Camera.FieldOfView or 70,
    UserName = LocalPlayer and LocalPlayer.Name or "Player",

}

-- // Populate Initial Flags (Strictly OFF / false by default), Values, Colors & Keybinds
for _, tab in ipairs(UI_SCHEMA) do
    for _, group in ipairs(tab.groups) do
        for _, item in ipairs(group.items) do
            if item.type == "checkbox" then
                GlobalEnv.OnyWtf.Flags[item.id] = (item.default == true)
            end
            if item.colors and #item.colors > 0 then
                GlobalEnv.OnyWtf.Colors[item.id] = item.colors
            end
            if item.keybind then
                GlobalEnv.OnyWtf.Keybinds[item.id] = item.keybind
            end
            if item.subSettings then
                for _, sub in ipairs(item.subSettings) do
                    GlobalEnv.OnyWtf.Values[sub.id] = sub.default
                end
            end
        end
    end
end

local Flags = GlobalEnv.OnyWtf.Flags
local Values = GlobalEnv.OnyWtf.Values
local ItemColors = GlobalEnv.OnyWtf.Colors
local ItemKeybinds = GlobalEnv.OnyWtf.Keybinds
local Telemetry = GlobalEnv.OnyWtf.Telemetry
local AccentColor = GlobalEnv.OnyWtf.Meta.Accent

Ony.Flags = Flags
Ony.Values = Values
Ony.ItemColors = ItemColors
Ony.ItemKeybinds = ItemKeybinds
Ony.Telemetry = Telemetry
Ony.AccentColor = AccentColor

Ony.FlagVisualSyncCallbacks = {}
Ony.SubValueVisualSyncCallbacks = {}

local function SetFlagState(flagId, newState)
    Flags[flagId] = (newState == true)
    if Ony.FlagVisualSyncCallbacks[flagId] then
        pcall(Ony.FlagVisualSyncCallbacks[flagId], Flags[flagId])
    end
    return Flags[flagId]
end
Ony.SetFlagState = SetFlagState

-- ============================================================================
-- SECTION 2: VECTOR MATHEMATICS, 3D PROJECTION & GEOMETRY ENGINE
-- ============================================================================
local MathEngine = {}
Ony.MathEngine = MathEngine

function MathEngine.Clamp(val, low, high)
    if val < low then
        return low
    end
    if val > high then
        return high
    end
    return val
end

function MathEngine.Lerp(a, b, t)
    return a + (b - a) * MathEngine.Clamp(t, 0, 1)
end

function MathEngine.LerpVector3(v1, v2, t)
    local alpha = MathEngine.Clamp(t, 0, 1)
    return v1:Lerp(v2, alpha)
end

function MathEngine.LerpColor3(c1, c2, t)
    local alpha = MathEngine.Clamp(t, 0, 1)
    return c1:Lerp(c2, alpha)
end

function MathEngine.WorldToScreen(worldPos)
    if not Camera then
        return Vector2.zero, false, 0
    end
    local viewportPoint, onScreen = Camera:WorldToViewportPoint(worldPos)
    return Vector2.new(viewportPoint.X, viewportPoint.Y), onScreen, viewportPoint.Z
end

function MathEngine.GetDistanceFromLocal(worldPos)
    local char = LocalPlayer and LocalPlayer.Character
    local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not hrp then
        if Camera then
            return (Camera.CFrame.Position - worldPos).Magnitude
        end
        return 0
    end
    return (hrp.Position - worldPos).Magnitude
end

function MathEngine.GetPingSeconds()
    local ok, ping = pcall(function()
        if LocalPlayer and type(LocalPlayer.GetNetworkPing) == "function" then
            return LocalPlayer:GetNetworkPing()
        end
        return 0.065
    end)
    if ok and type(ping) == "number" and ping > 0 then
        return MathEngine.Clamp(ping, 0.015, 0.45)
    end
    return 0.065
end

function MathEngine.Get3DBoundingBoxCorners(cf, sizeVec)
    local hx = sizeVec.X * 0.5
    local hy = sizeVec.Y * 0.5
    local hz = sizeVec.Z * 0.5
    return {
        cf * Vector3.new(-hx,  hy, -hz),
        cf * Vector3.new( hx,  hy, -hz),
        cf * Vector3.new( hx,  hy,  hz),
        cf * Vector3.new(-hx,  hy,  hz),
        cf * Vector3.new(-hx, -hy, -hz),
        cf * Vector3.new( hx, -hy, -hz),
        cf * Vector3.new( hx, -hy,  hz),
        cf * Vector3.new(-hx, -hy,  hz),
    }
end

MathEngine.Box3DEdgePairs = {
    { 1, 2 }, { 2, 3 }, { 3, 4 }, { 4, 1 },
    { 5, 6 }, { 6, 7 }, { 7, 8 }, { 8, 5 },
    { 1, 5 }, { 2, 6 }, { 3, 7 }, { 4, 8 },
}

MathEngine.R15BonePairs = {
    { "Head", "UpperTorso" },
    { "UpperTorso", "LowerTorso" },
    { "UpperTorso", "LeftUpperArm" },
    { "LeftUpperArm", "LeftLowerArm" },
    { "LeftLowerArm", "LeftHand" },
    { "UpperTorso", "RightUpperArm" },
    { "RightUpperArm", "RightLowerArm" },
    { "RightLowerArm", "RightHand" },
    { "LowerTorso", "LeftUpperLeg" },
    { "LeftUpperLeg", "LeftLowerLeg" },
    { "LeftLowerLeg", "LeftFoot" },
    { "LowerTorso", "RightUpperLeg" },
    { "RightUpperLeg", "RightLowerLeg" },
    { "RightLowerLeg", "RightFoot" },
}

MathEngine.R6BonePairs = {
    { "Head", "Torso" },
    { "Torso", "Left Arm" },
    { "Torso", "Right Arm" },
    { "Torso", "Left Leg" },
    { "Torso", "Right Leg" },
}

function MathEngine.GetCharacterBoneSegments(character)
    if not character then
        return {}
    end
    local isR15 = (character:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso") ~= nil)
    local pairsList = isR15 and MathEngine.R15BonePairs or MathEngine.R6BonePairs
    local segments = {}

    for _, pair in ipairs(pairsList) do
        local pA = character:FindFirstChild(pair[1])
        local pB = character:FindFirstChild(pair[2])
        if pA and pB and pA:IsA("BasePart") and pB:IsA("BasePart") then
            local sA, onA = MathEngine.WorldToScreen(pA.Position)
            local sB, onB = MathEngine.WorldToScreen(pB.Position)
            if onA and onB then
                table.insert(segments, { A = sA, B = sB })
            end
        end
    end
    return segments
end

function MathEngine.GetOffScreenArrowTriangle(worldPos, radiusFromCenter, arrowSize)
    if not Camera then
        return nil
    end
    local vp = Camera.ViewportSize
    local center = vp * 0.5
    local rel = Camera.CFrame:PointToObjectSpace(worldPos)
    local angle = math.atan2(-rel.Y, rel.X)

    local tipDist = radiusFromCenter + arrowSize * 0.6
    local baseDist = radiusFromCenter - arrowSize * 0.5

    local dir = Vector2.new(math.cos(angle), math.sin(angle))
    local perp = Vector2.new(-dir.Y, dir.X)

    local tip = center + dir * tipDist
    local baseCenter = center + dir * baseDist
    local leftWing = baseCenter + perp * (arrowSize * 0.55)
    local rightWing = baseCenter - perp * (arrowSize * 0.55)

    return {
        Tip = tip,
        Left = leftWing,
        Right = rightWing,
        Center = center + dir * radiusFromCenter,
        AngleDeg = math.deg(angle),
    }
end

function MathEngine.ComputeKnifeThrowArc(originPos, lookDir, speed, gravity, stepCount)
    local points = {}
    local dt = 0.045
    local gVec = Vector3.new(0, -math.abs(gravity or 95), 0)
    local vel = lookDir.Unit * (speed or 115)
    local currPos = originPos

    local rayParams = RaycastParams.new()
    rayParams.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
    rayParams.IgnoreWater = true
    if LocalPlayer and LocalPlayer.Character then
        rayParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = { LocalPlayer.Character }
    end

    table.insert(points, currPos)
    local hitPosition = nil

    for _ = 1, (stepCount or 16) do
        local nextVel = vel + gVec * dt
        local nextPos = currPos + ((vel + nextVel) * 0.5) * dt
        local segDelta = nextPos - currPos
        local rayResult = Workspace:Raycast(currPos, segDelta, rayParams)
        if rayResult then
            table.insert(points, rayResult.Position)
            hitPosition = rayResult.Position
            break
        end
        table.insert(points, nextPos)
        currPos = nextPos
        vel = nextVel
    end

    return points, hitPosition or currPos
end

function MathEngine.IsPartVisible(targetPart, ignoreList)
    if not Camera or not targetPart then
        return false
    end
    local origin = Camera.CFrame.Position
    local direction = targetPart.Position - origin
    if direction.Magnitude < 1 then
        return true
    end

    local params = RaycastParams.new()
    params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
    params.IgnoreWater = true

    local filter = { Camera }
    if LocalPlayer and LocalPlayer.Character then
        table.insert(filter, LocalPlayer.Character)
    end
    if type(ignoreList) == "table" then
        for _, item in ipairs(ignoreList) do
            table.insert(filter, item)
        end
    end
    params.FilterDescendantsInstances = filter

    local result = Workspace:Raycast(origin, direction, params)
    if not result then
        return true
    end
    if targetPart.Parent and result.Instance:IsDescendantOf(targetPart.Parent) then
        return true
    end
    return false
end

function MathEngine.ResolveHitboxPart(character, preference)
    if not character then
        return nil
    end
    local pref = tostring(preference or "Head")
    if pref == "Head" then
        return character:FindFirstChild("Head") or character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    elseif pref == "HumanoidRootPart" then
        return character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or character:FindFirstChild("Head")
    elseif pref == "Torso" then
        return character:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
            or character:FindFirstChild("Torso")
            or character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    elseif pref == "Legs" then
        return character:FindFirstChild("RightUpperLeg")
            or character:FindFirstChild("Right Leg")
            or character:FindFirstChild("LowerTorso")
            or character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    elseif pref == "Arms" then
        return character:FindFirstChild("RightUpperArm")
            or character:FindFirstChild("Right Arm")
            or character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    end
    return character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or character:FindFirstChild("Head")
end

function MathEngine.PredictTargetPosition(targetPart, shotMode, yOffset)
    if not targetPart then
        return Vector3.zero
    end
    local basePos = targetPart.Position
    local vel = targetPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity or Vector3.zero
    local mode = tostring(shotMode or "Ping Prediction")
    local yOff = tonumber(yOffset) or 0

    if mode == "Default" then
        return basePos + Vector3.new(0, yOff, 0)
    elseif mode == "Teleport Bullet" then
        local lead = vel * 0.045
        return basePos + lead + Vector3.new(0, yOff, 0)
    else
        local ping = MathEngine.GetPingSeconds()
        local leadFactor = MathEngine.Clamp(ping * 1.65 + 0.085, 0.10, 0.36)
        return basePos + (vel * leadFactor) + Vector3.new(0, yOff, 0)
    end
end


-- ============================================================================
-- SECTION 3: SCREEN OVERLAY, TOAST NOTIFICATIONS & AUDIO ALERT ENGINE
-- ============================================================================
do
    local ScreenGui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
    ScreenGui.Name = GUI_NAME
    ScreenGui.ResetOnSpawn = false
    ScreenGui.IgnoreGuiInset = true
    ScreenGui.DisplayOrder = 999999
    ScreenGui.Enabled = true
    ScreenGui.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Global
    ParentGuiSafely(ScreenGui)
    Ony.ScreenGui = ScreenGui

    local VectorOverlay = Instance.new("Frame")
    VectorOverlay.Name = "OnyVectorOverlay"
    VectorOverlay.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
    VectorOverlay.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    VectorOverlay.Active = false
    VectorOverlay.ZIndex = 5
    VectorOverlay.Parent = ScreenGui
    Ony.VectorOverlay = VectorOverlay

    function Ony.CreateGuiLine(parent, thickness, color, zIndex)
        local line = Instance.new("Frame")
        line.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
        line.BorderSizePixel = 0
        line.BackgroundColor3 = color or AccentColor
        line.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, thickness or 1.5)
        line.ZIndex = zIndex or 5
        line.Visible = false
        line.Parent = parent or VectorOverlay
        return line
    end

    function Ony.SetGuiLine(line, p1, p2, thickness, color, transparency)
        if not line then
            return
        end
        local delta = p2 - p1
        local dist = delta.Magnitude
        if dist < 0.5 then
            line.Visible = false
            return
        end
        local mid = (p1 + p2) * 0.5
        local angle = math.deg(math.atan2(delta.Y, delta.X))
        line.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(mid.X, mid.Y)
        line.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(dist, thickness or 1.5)
        line.Rotation = angle
        if color then
            line.BackgroundColor3 = color
        end
        if transparency ~= nil then
            line.BackgroundTransparency = transparency
        end
        line.Visible = true
    end

    local ToastContainer = Instance.new("Frame")
    ToastContainer.Name = "OnyToastContainer"
    ToastContainer.Size = UDim2.new(0, 285, 1, -40)
    ToastContainer.Position = UDim2.new(1, -300, 0, 18)
    ToastContainer.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    ToastContainer.ZIndex = 130
    ToastContainer.Parent = ScreenGui
    Ony.ToastContainer = ToastContainer

    local ToastLayout = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
    ToastLayout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
    ToastLayout.VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Top
    ToastLayout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 6)
    ToastLayout.Parent = ToastContainer

    function Ony.PlayFeedbackBeep(pitch)
        pcall(function()
            if not SoundService then
                return
            end
            local snd = Instance.new("Sound")
            snd.SoundId = "rbxassetid://4590662766"
            snd.Volume = 0.35
            snd.PlaybackSpeed = pitch or 1.05
            snd.Parent = SoundService
            snd:Play()
            task.delay(1.2, function()
                pcall(function()
                    snd:Destroy()
                end)
            end)
        end)
    end

    function Ony.NotifyToast(title, message, customColor)
        if not ToastContainer or not ToastContainer.Parent then
            return
        end
        local strokeCol = customColor or AccentColor
        local card = Instance.new("Frame")
        card.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 48)
        card.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(20, 16, 29)
        card.BorderSizePixel = 0
        card.ZIndex = 131
        card.Parent = ToastContainer
        Instance.new("UICorner", card).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)

        local st = Instance.new("UIStroke", card)
        st.Color = strokeCol
        st.Thickness = 1.3

        local sideBar = Instance.new("Frame")
        sideBar.Size = UDim2.new(0, 3, 1, -12)
        sideBar.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(6, 6)
        sideBar.BackgroundColor3 = strokeCol
        sideBar.BorderSizePixel = 0
        sideBar.ZIndex = 132
        sideBar.Parent = card
        Instance.new("UICorner", sideBar).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)

        local tl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        tl.Size = UDim2.new(1, -24, 0, 16)
        tl.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(16, 6)
        tl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        tl.Text = tostring(title or "Ony.wtf MM2")
        tl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        tl.TextSize = 11
        tl.TextColor3 = strokeCol
        tl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        tl.ZIndex = 132
        tl.Parent = card

        local ml = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        ml.Size = UDim2.new(1, -24, 0, 18)
        ml.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(16, 23)
        ml.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        ml.Text = tostring(message or "")
        ml.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
        ml.TextSize = 11
        ml.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(238, 234, 245)
        ml.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        ml.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
        ml.ZIndex = 132
        ml.Parent = card

        task.delay(2.8, function()
            if card and card.Parent then
                TweenService:Create(card, TweenInfo.new(0.26, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad), {
                    BackgroundTransparency = 1
                }):Play()
                task.wait(0.26)
                pcall(function()
                    card:Destroy()
                end)
            end
        end)
    end
end

-- ============================================================================
-- SECTION 4: DEEP MURDER MYSTERY 2 (MM2) ROLE, MAP & REMOTE ENGINE
-- ============================================================================
do
    Ony.PlayerRoles = {}
    Ony.PlayerAliveStatus = {}
    Ony.PlayerCoinCounts = {}
    Ony.LastExposedRoundId = 0
    Ony.CurrentRoundId = 1

    local KnownMM2MapNames = {
        Bank2 = true,
        BioLab = true,
        Factory = true,
        Hospital3 = true,
        Hotel2 = true,
        House2 = true,
        Mansion2 = true,
        MilBase = true,
        Office3 = true,
        PoliceStation = true,
        ResearchFacility = true,
        Workplace = true,
        VampireCastle = true,
        Yacht = true,
        Barn = true,
        BeachResort = true,
        ChristmasInItaly = true,
        IceCastle = true,
        LogCabin = true,
        SkiLodge = true,
        TrainStation = true,
        Workshop = true,
        Manor = true,
        Farmhouse = true,
        Mineshaft = true,
    }

    function Ony.LocateMM2Remotes()
        pcall(function()
            if not Ony.GetPlayerDataRemote then
                Ony.GetPlayerDataRemote = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("GetPlayerData", true)
            end
            if not Ony.CoinCollectedRemote then
                Ony.CoinCollectedRemote = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("CoinCollected", true)
            end
            if not Ony.RoleSelectRemote then
                Ony.RoleSelectRemote = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("RoleSelect", true)
            end
            if not Ony.RoundStartRemote then
                Ony.RoundStartRemote = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("RoundStart", true)
            end
            if not Ony.RoundEndRemote then
                Ony.RoundEndRemote = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("RoundEndFade", true)
                    or ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Fade", true)
            end
            if not Ony.BoxControllerRemote then
                Ony.BoxControllerRemote = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("BoxController", true)
            end
        end)
    end
    Ony.LocateMM2Remotes()

    function Ony.GetPlayerWeaponLabel(plr)
        if not plr then
            return "Unarmed"
        end
        local char = plr.Character
        local bp = plr:FindFirstChild("Backpack")

        if char then
            local eqTool = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
            if eqTool then
                if eqTool.Name == "Knife" or eqTool:FindFirstChild("Stab") or eqTool:FindFirstChild("Slash") then
                    return "Knife [Equipped]"
                elseif eqTool.Name == "Gun" or eqTool.Name == "Revolver" or eqTool:FindFirstChild("CreateBeam", true) then
                    return "Gun [Equipped]"
                else
                    return eqTool.Name .. " [Equipped]"
                end
            end
        end

        if bp then
            local knife = bp:FindFirstChild("Knife")
            if knife then
                return "Knife [Holstered]"
            end
            local gun = bp:FindFirstChild("Gun") or bp:FindFirstChild("Revolver")
            if gun then
                return "Gun [Holstered]"
            end
            local anyTool = bp:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
            if anyTool then
                return anyTool.Name
            end
        end

        return "Unarmed"
    end

    function Ony.HasKnifeTool(plr)
        if not plr then
            return false, nil
        end
        local char = plr.Character
        local bp = plr:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
        if char then
            local k = char:FindFirstChild("Knife")
            if k and k:IsA("Tool") then
                return true, k
            end
            for _, tool in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
                if tool:IsA("Tool") and (tool:FindFirstChild("Stab") or tool:FindFirstChild("Slash") or tool:FindFirstChild("Throw")) then
                    return true, tool
                end
            end
        end
        if bp then
            local k = bp:FindFirstChild("Knife")
            if k and k:IsA("Tool") then
                return true, k
            end
            for _, tool in ipairs(bp:GetChildren()) do
                if tool:IsA("Tool") and (tool:FindFirstChild("Stab") or tool:FindFirstChild("Slash") or tool:FindFirstChild("Throw")) then
                    return true, tool
                end
            end
        end
        return false, nil
    end

    function Ony.HasGunTool(plr)
        if not plr then
            return false, nil
        end
        local char = plr.Character
        local bp = plr:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
        if char then
            local g = char:FindFirstChild("Gun") or char:FindFirstChild("Revolver")
            if g and g:IsA("Tool") then
                return true, g
            end
            for _, tool in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
                if tool:IsA("Tool") and tool:FindFirstChild("CreateBeam", true) then
                    return true, tool
                end
            end
        end
        if bp then
            local g = bp:FindFirstChild("Gun") or bp:FindFirstChild("Revolver")
            if g and g:IsA("Tool") then
                return true, g
            end
            for _, tool in ipairs(bp:GetChildren()) do
                if tool:IsA("Tool") and tool:FindFirstChild("CreateBeam", true) then
                    return true, tool
                end
            end
        end
        return false, nil
    end

    function Ony.ResolvePlayerRole(plr)
        if not plr then
            return "Innocent"
        end
        local hasKnife = Ony.HasKnifeTool(plr)
        if hasKnife then
            Ony.PlayerRoles[plr.Name] = "Murderer"
            return "Murderer"
        end
        local hasGun = Ony.HasGunTool(plr)
        if hasGun then
            if Ony.PlayerRoles[plr.Name] == "Hero" then
                return "Hero"
            end
            if Ony.PlayerRoles[plr.Name] ~= "Sheriff" then
                Ony.PlayerRoles[plr.Name] = "Hero"
                return "Hero"
            end
            return "Sheriff"
        end
        if Ony.PlayerRoles[plr.Name] then
            return Ony.PlayerRoles[plr.Name]
        end
        return "Innocent"
    end

    function Ony.IsPlayerInActiveRound(plr)
        if not plr or not plr.Character then
            return false
        end
        local hum = plr.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        local hrp = plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not hum or not hrp or hum.Health <= 0 then
            return false
        end
        if Ony.PlayerAliveStatus[plr.Name] == false then
            return false
        end
        return true
    end

    function Ony.GetRoleColor(role)
        if role == "Murderer" then
            return (ItemColors["role_murderer_esp"] and ItemColors["role_murderer_esp"][1]) or Color3.fromRGB(255, 42, 75)
        elseif role == "Sheriff" then
            return (ItemColors["role_sheriff_esp"] and ItemColors["role_sheriff_esp"][1]) or Color3.fromRGB(42, 127, 255)
        elseif role == "Hero" then
            return (ItemColors["role_hero_esp"] and ItemColors["role_hero_esp"][1]) or Color3.fromRGB(255, 213, 42)
        elseif role == "Zombie" then
            return Color3.fromRGB(42, 255, 101)
        end
        return (ItemColors["role_innocent_esp"] and ItemColors["role_innocent_esp"][1]) or Color3.fromRGB(42, 255, 101)
    end

    function Ony.RefreshMM2Roles()
        Ony.LocateMM2Remotes()
        local gpd = Ony.GetPlayerDataRemote
        if gpd and gpd:IsA("RemoteFunction") then
            local ok, data = pcall(function()
                return gpd:InvokeServer()
            end)
            if ok and type(data) == "table" then
                local foundMurderer = "None"
                local foundSheriff = "None"
                local foundHero = "None"
                local activeRoleFound = false

                for playerName, info in pairs(data) do
                    if type(info) == "table" then
                        local isDead = (info.Dead == true) or (info.Killed == true)
                        Ony.PlayerAliveStatus[playerName] = not isDead
                        if type(info.Coins) == "number" then
                            Ony.PlayerCoinCounts[playerName] = info.Coins
                            if playerName == LocalPlayer.Name then
                                Telemetry.CoinsCollected = info.Coins
                            end
                        end
                        if not isDead and type(info.Role) == "string" and info.Role ~= "" then
                            Ony.PlayerRoles[playerName] = info.Role
                            if info.Role == "Murderer" then
                                foundMurderer = playerName
                                activeRoleFound = true
                            elseif info.Role == "Sheriff" then
                                foundSheriff = playerName
                                activeRoleFound = true
                            elseif info.Role == "Hero" then
                                foundHero = playerName
                                activeRoleFound = true
                            end
                        else
                            Ony.PlayerRoles[playerName] = "Innocent"
                        end
                    end
                end

                Telemetry.MurdererName = foundMurderer
                Telemetry.SheriffName = foundSheriff
                Telemetry.HeroName = foundHero
                Telemetry.CurrentRole = Ony.ResolvePlayerRole(LocalPlayer)

                if activeRoleFound then
                    Telemetry.LastRoundTimestamp = os.clock()
                    if Ony.LastExposedRoundId ~= Ony.CurrentRoundId then
                        Ony.LastExposedRoundId = Ony.CurrentRoundId
                        if Flags["auto_expose_murderer"] and foundMurderer ~= "None" and Ony.SendChatMessage then
                            Ony.SendChatMessage("[Ony.wtf] Murderer is: " .. foundMurderer)
                        end
                        if Flags["auto_expose_sheriff"] and foundSheriff ~= "None" and Ony.SendChatMessage then
                            Ony.SendChatMessage("[Ony.wtf] Sheriff is: " .. foundSheriff)
                        end
                        if Flags["webhook_role_assigned"] and Ony.SendWebhookEvent then
                            Ony.SendWebhookEvent(
                                "MM2 Round Role Assigned",
                                string.format(
                                    "Your Role: **%s** | Murderer: **%s** | Sheriff: **%s**",
                                    tostring(Telemetry.CurrentRole),
                                    tostring(foundMurderer),
                                    tostring(foundSheriff)
                                )
                            )
                        end
                    end
                end
                return
            end
        end

        -- Fallback scan via tools in Character / Backpack
        local foundMurderer = "None"
        local foundSheriff = "None"
        for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            local r = Ony.ResolvePlayerRole(plr)
            Ony.PlayerRoles[plr.Name] = r
            if r == "Murderer" then
                foundMurderer = plr.Name
            elseif r == "Sheriff" or r == "Hero" then
                foundSheriff = plr.Name
            end
        end
        Telemetry.MurdererName = foundMurderer
        Telemetry.SheriffName = foundSheriff
        Telemetry.CurrentRole = Ony.ResolvePlayerRole(LocalPlayer)
    end

    pcall(function()
        if Ony.CoinCollectedRemote and Ony.CoinCollectedRemote:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
            TrackConnection(Ony.CoinCollectedRemote.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(_, currentCoins, maxCoins)
                if type(currentCoins) == "number" then
                    Telemetry.CoinsCollected = currentCoins
                else
                    Telemetry.CoinsCollected = math.min(Telemetry.BagCapacity, Telemetry.CoinsCollected + 1)
                end
                if type(maxCoins) == "number" and maxCoins > 0 then
                    Telemetry.BagCapacity = maxCoins
                end
            end))
        end
        if Ony.RoundStartRemote and Ony.RoundStartRemote:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
            TrackConnection(Ony.RoundStartRemote.OnClientEvent:Connect(function()
                Ony.CurrentRoundId = Ony.CurrentRoundId + 1
                Telemetry.RoundsPlayed = Telemetry.RoundsPlayed + 1
                Telemetry.CoinsCollected = 0
                Telemetry.LastRoundTimestamp = os.clock()
                task.delay(1.2, Ony.RefreshMM2Roles)
            end))
        end
    end)

    function Ony.GetMurdererPlayer()
        for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if plr ~= LocalPlayer and Ony.ResolvePlayerRole(plr) == "Murderer" and Ony.IsPlayerInActiveRound(plr) then
                return plr
            end
        end
        return nil
    end

    function Ony.GetSheriffPlayer()
        for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if plr ~= LocalPlayer then
                local r = Ony.ResolvePlayerRole(plr)
                if (r == "Sheriff" or r == "Hero") and Ony.IsPlayerInActiveRound(plr) then
                    return plr
                end
            end
        end
        return nil
    end

    function Ony.GetClosestAlivePlayerToPoint(worldPos, roleFilter)
        local bestPlr = nil
        local bestDist = math.huge
        for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if plr ~= LocalPlayer and Ony.IsPlayerInActiveRound(plr) then
                local r = Ony.ResolvePlayerRole(plr)
                local roleOk = (not roleFilter)
                    or (roleFilter == "Murderer" and r == "Murderer")
                    or (roleFilter == "Sheriff" and (r == "Sheriff" or r == "Hero"))
                    or (roleFilter == "Innocent" and r == "Innocent")
                if roleOk then
                    local hrp = plr.Character and plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    if hrp then
                        local d = (hrp.Position - worldPos).Magnitude
                        if d < bestDist then
                            bestDist = d
                            bestPlr = plr
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        return bestPlr, bestDist
    end

    Ony.CachedMapWalls = {}
    Ony.XRayActiveState = false

    function Ony.FindActiveMap()
        if Ony.CachedActiveMap and Ony.CachedActiveMap.Parent == Workspace and (Ony.CachedActiveMap:FindFirstChild("CoinContainer") or Ony.CachedActiveMap:FindFirstChild("Spawns")) then
            return Ony.CachedActiveMap
        end
        for _, child in ipairs(Workspace:GetChildren()) do
            if child:IsA("Model") or child:IsA("Folder") then
                if child:FindFirstChild("CoinContainer") or child:FindFirstChild("Spawns") or KnownMM2MapNames[child.Name] then
                    Ony.CachedActiveMap = child
                    Telemetry.ActiveMapName = child.Name
                    Ony.CachedMapWalls = {}
                    for _, desc in ipairs(child:GetDescendants()) do
                        if desc:IsA("BasePart") and desc.Transparency < 0.85 and not desc:IsDescendantOf(child:FindFirstChild("CoinContainer") or Ony.ScreenGui) then
                            table.insert(Ony.CachedMapWalls, desc)
                        end
                    end
                    return child
                end
            end
        end
        Telemetry.ActiveMapName = "Lobby"
        return nil
    end

    function Ony.UpdateXRayMapWalls()
        local shouldXRay = (Flags["xray_walls"] == true)
        local opacityPct = tonumber(Values["xray_opacity"]) or 60
        local modifier = MathEngine.Clamp(opacityPct / 100, 0.1, 0.92)

        if shouldXRay then
            local map = Ony.FindActiveMap()
            if map then
                Ony.XRayActiveState = true
                for _, part in ipairs(Ony.CachedMapWalls) do
                    if part and part.Parent then
                        part.LocalTransparencyModifier = modifier
                    end
                end
            end
        elseif Ony.XRayActiveState then
            Ony.XRayActiveState = false
            for _, part in ipairs(Ony.CachedMapWalls) do
                if part and part.Parent then
                    part.LocalTransparencyModifier = 0
                end
            end
        end
    end

    function Ony.FindBestCoinTarget()
        local map = Ony.FindActiveMap()
        if not map then
            return nil, 0
        end
        local container = map:FindFirstChild("CoinContainer")
        if not container then
            return nil, 0
        end

        local char = LocalPlayer.Character
        local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not hrp then
            return nil, 0
        end

        local murderer = Ony.GetMurdererPlayer()
        local mHrp = murderer and murderer.Character and murderer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

        local validCoins = {}
        for _, coin in ipairs(container:GetChildren()) do
            local part = coin:IsA("BasePart") and coin or coin:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")
            if part and part.Parent and part.Transparency < 0.95 then
                local hasTouch = part:FindFirstChildOfClass("TouchTransmitter") or part:FindFirstChild("TouchInterest") or coin:FindFirstChild("TouchInterest", true)
                if hasTouch ~= nil or coin.Name == "Coin_Server" or coin.Name == "Coin" or coin.Name == "SnowToken" or coin.Name == "Egg" or coin.Name == "Candy" or coin.Name == "BeachBall" then
                    table.insert(validCoins, part)
                end
            end
        end

        if #validCoins == 0 then
            return nil, 0
        end

        local prioritizeClusters = (Flags["prioritize_coin_clusters"] == true)
        local bestPart = nil
        local bestScore = math.huge

        for _, part in ipairs(validCoins) do
            local dist = (hrp.Position - part.Position).Magnitude
            local score = dist

            if mHrp then
                local distToMurderer = (mHrp.Position - part.Position).Magnitude
                if distToMurderer < 18 then
                    score = score + 220
                elseif distToMurderer < 30 then
                    score = score + 75
                end
            end

            if prioritizeClusters then
                local neighbors = 0
                for _, other in ipairs(validCoins) do
                    if other ~= part and (other.Position - part.Position).Magnitude <= 26 then
                        neighbors = neighbors + 1
                    end
                end
                score = score - (neighbors * 9.5)
            end

            if score < bestScore then
                bestScore = score
                bestPart = part
            end
        end

        if bestPart then
            return bestPart, (hrp.Position - bestPart.Position).Magnitude
        end
        return nil, 0
    end
end

-- ============================================================================
-- SECTION 5: ADVANCED AIMBOT, SILENT AIM (__NAMECALL) & COMBAT AUTOMATION
-- ============================================================================
do
    Ony.AimbotToggleKeyState = true
    Ony.CurrentLockedTarget = nil
    Ony.LastAutoGunShotClock = 0
    Ony.LastAutoKnifeThrowClock = 0
    Ony.HitboxExpandedState = false

    function Ony.SpawnBulletImpactBeam(originPos, targetPos, beamColor)
        if not Flags["bullet_impacts"] then
            return
        end
        pcall(function()
            local dist = (targetPos - originPos).Magnitude
            local mid = (originPos + targetPos) * 0.5

            local beamPart = Instance.new("Part")
            beamPart.Name = "OnyBulletBeam"
            beamPart.Anchored = true
            beamPart.CanCollide = false
            beamPart.CanQuery = false
            beamPart.Material = Enum.Material.Neon
            beamPart.Color = beamColor or (ItemColors["bullet_impacts"] and ItemColors["bullet_impacts"][1]) or Color3.fromRGB(56, 189, 248)
            beamPart.Transparency = 0.15
            beamPart.Size = Vector3.new(0.16, 0.16, dist)
            beamPart.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(mid, targetPos)
            beamPart.Parent = Workspace
            TrackWorldInstance(beamPart)

            local hitBox = Instance.new("Part")
            hitBox.Name = "OnyBulletImpactMarker"
            hitBox.Anchored = true
            hitBox.CanCollide = false
            hitBox.CanQuery = false
            hitBox.Material = Enum.Material.Neon
            hitBox.Color = AccentColor
            hitBox.Transparency = 0.1
            hitBox.Size = Vector3.new(1.1, 1.1, 1.1)
            hitBox.CFrame = CFrame.new(targetPos)
            hitBox.Parent = Workspace
            TrackWorldInstance(hitBox)

            task.delay(0.85, function()
                if beamPart and beamPart.Parent then
                    TweenService:Create(beamPart, TweenInfo.new(0.35), { Transparency = 1 }):Play()
                end
                if hitBox and hitBox.Parent then
                    TweenService:Create(hitBox, TweenInfo.new(0.35), { Transparency = 1, Size = Vector3.new(2.2, 2.2, 2.2) }):Play()
                end
                task.wait(0.38)
                pcall(function() beamPart:Destroy() end)
                pcall(function() hitBox:Destroy() end)
            end)
        end)
    end

    function Ony.ResolveCombatTarget(useSilentFov)
        if not Camera then
            return nil, nil, nil
        end

        if Flags["aimbot_lock_murderer"] then
            local m = Ony.GetMurdererPlayer()
            if m and m.Character then
                local part = MathEngine.ResolveHitboxPart(m.Character, Values["aimbot_hitbox"])
                if part and (not Flags["wall_check"] or MathEngine.IsPartVisible(part)) then
                    local pred = MathEngine.PredictTargetPosition(part, Values["shot_mode"], Values["shoot_y_offset"])
                    return m, part, pred
                end
            end
        end

        if Flags["aimbot_lock_sheriff"] then
            local s = Ony.GetSheriffPlayer()
            if s and s.Character then
                local part = MathEngine.ResolveHitboxPart(s.Character, Values["aimbot_hitbox"])
                if part and (not Flags["wall_check"] or MathEngine.IsPartVisible(part)) then
                    local pred = MathEngine.PredictTargetPosition(part, Values["shot_mode"], Values["shoot_y_offset"])
                    return s, part, pred
                end
            end
        end

        local myRole = Ony.ResolvePlayerRole(LocalPlayer)
        local refCenter = Camera.ViewportSize * 0.5
        local maxFovRadius = useSilentFov
            and (tonumber(Values["silent_aim_fov"]) or 180)
            or (tonumber(Values["fov_radius"]) or 110)
        local enforceFov = useSilentFov or (Values["fov_lock"] ~= false)

        local bestPlayer = nil
        local bestPart = nil
        local bestScore = math.huge

        for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if plr ~= LocalPlayer and Ony.IsPlayerInActiveRound(plr) then
                local targetRole = Ony.ResolvePlayerRole(plr)
                local validTarget = true

                if (myRole == "Sheriff" or myRole == "Hero" or myRole == "Innocent") and targetRole ~= "Murderer" then
                    if Ony.GetMurdererPlayer() ~= nil then
                        validTarget = false
                    end
                end

                if validTarget and plr.Character then
                    local part = MathEngine.ResolveHitboxPart(plr.Character, Values["aimbot_hitbox"])
                    if part then
                        local visibleOk = (not Flags["wall_check"]) or MathEngine.IsPartVisible(part)
                        if visibleOk then
                            local screenPt, onScreen = MathEngine.WorldToScreen(part.Position)
                            if onScreen then
                                local pixelDist = (screenPt - refCenter).Magnitude
                                if (not enforceFov) or (pixelDist <= maxFovRadius) then
                                    local score = pixelDist
                                    if targetRole == "Murderer" then
                                        score = score - 500
                                    end
                                    if score < bestScore then
                                        bestScore = score
                                        bestPlayer = plr
                                        bestPart = part
                                    end
                                end
                            elseif not enforceFov then
                                local worldDist = MathEngine.GetDistanceFromLocal(part.Position)
                                if worldDist < bestScore then
                                    bestScore = worldDist
                                    bestPlayer = plr
                                    bestPart = part
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        if bestPlayer and bestPart then
            local pred = MathEngine.PredictTargetPosition(bestPart, Values["shot_mode"], Values["shoot_y_offset"])
            return bestPlayer, bestPart, pred
        end

        return nil, nil, nil
    end

    -- Optional __namecall Silent Aim Hook (Safely Skipped in Standard LocalScripts)
    pcall(function()
        if ExecutorCaps.HasHookMetaMethod then
            local oldNamecall
            oldNamecall = hookmetamethod(game, "__namecall", function(self, ...)
                local method = (type(getnamecallmethod) == "function" and getnamecallmethod()) or ""
                local args = { ... }

                if Flags["silent_aim_toggle"] and not (type(checkcaller) == "function" and checkcaller()) then
                    if method == "InvokeServer" and tostring(self.Name) == "RemoteFunction" and self.Parent and tostring(self.Parent.Name) == "CreateBeam" then
                        local targetPlr, targetPart, predictedPos = Ony.ResolveCombatTarget(true)
                        if targetPlr and targetPart and predictedPos then
                            args[2] = predictedPos
                            if LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                                Ony.SpawnBulletImpactBeam(LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position, predictedPos)
                            end
                            if Flags["shot_info"] then
                                task.defer(function()
                                    Ony.NotifyToast(
                                        "Silent Aim Hit",
                                        string.format("Redirected Beam -> %s (%s)", targetPlr.Name, tostring(targetPart.Name))
                                    )
                                end)
                            end
                            return oldNamecall(self, unpack(args))
                        end
                    elseif method == "FireServer" and tostring(self.Name) == "Throw" then
                        local targetPlr, targetPart, predictedPos = Ony.ResolveCombatTarget(true)
                        if targetPlr and targetPart and predictedPos then
                            if typeof(args[1]) == "CFrame" then
                                args[1] = CFrame.new(predictedPos)
                                if typeof(args[2]) == "Vector3" then
                                    args[2] = predictedPos
                                end
                            elseif typeof(args[1]) == "Vector3" then
                                args[1] = predictedPos
                            end
                            return oldNamecall(self, unpack(args))
                        end
                    end
                end

                return oldNamecall(self, ...)
            end)
        end
    end)

    function Ony.ShootMurdererWithGun(requireVisible, isInstaWin)
        local target = Ony.GetMurdererPlayer()
        if not target or not target.Character then
            if not requireVisible then
                Ony.NotifyToast("Ony.wtf Sheriff", "No active Murderer found in round!")
            end
            return false
        end

        local part = MathEngine.ResolveHitboxPart(target.Character, Values["aimbot_hitbox"])
        if not part then
            return false
        end

        if requireVisible and not MathEngine.IsPartVisible(part) then
            return false
        end

        local char = LocalPlayer.Character
        local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not char or not hrp or not hum then
            return false
        end

        local hasGun, gunTool = Ony.HasGunTool(LocalPlayer)
        if not hasGun or not gunTool then
            if not requireVisible then
                Ony.NotifyToast("Ony.wtf Sheriff", "You do not have the Sheriff Gun!")
            end
            return false
        end

        local savedCFrame = hrp.CFrame
        if isInstaWin or tostring(Values["shot_mode"]) == "Teleport Bullet" then
            hrp.CFrame = part.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 2.5, 6.5)
            task.wait(0.05)
        end

        if gunTool.Parent ~= char then
            pcall(function()
                hum:EquipTool(gunTool)
            end)
            task.wait(0.06)
        end

        local predictedPos = MathEngine.PredictTargetPosition(part, Values["shot_mode"], Values["shoot_y_offset"])
        local createBeam = gunTool:FindFirstChild("CreateBeam", true)
        local remoteFunc = createBeam and createBeam:FindFirstChild("RemoteFunction")

        if remoteFunc and remoteFunc:IsA("RemoteFunction") then
            task.spawn(function()
                pcall(function()
                    remoteFunc:InvokeServer(1, predictedPos, "AH2")
                end)
            end)
            Ony.SpawnBulletImpactBeam(hrp.Position, predictedPos)
            if Flags["shot_info"] or not requireVisible then
                local dist = math.floor((hrp.Position - predictedPos).Magnitude)
                Ony.NotifyToast(
                    "Ony.wtf Sheriff Shot",
                    string.format("Fired at %s [%s | %d studs]", target.Name, part.Name, dist)
                )
            end
        else
            pcall(function()
                gunTool:Activate()
            end)
        end

        if isInstaWin or tostring(Values["shot_mode"]) == "Teleport Bullet" then
            task.wait(0.08)
            if hrp and hrp.Parent then
                hrp.CFrame = savedCFrame
            end
        end

        return true
    end

    function Ony.ExecuteKnifeCombat(filterRole)
        local char = LocalPlayer.Character
        local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not char or not hrp or not hum then
            return
        end

        local hasKnife, knifeTool = Ony.HasKnifeTool(LocalPlayer)
        if not hasKnife or not knifeTool then
            if filterRole then
                Ony.NotifyToast("Ony.wtf Murderer", "Knife tool not found in character or backpack!")
            end
            return
        end

        if knifeTool.Parent ~= char then
            pcall(function()
                hum:EquipTool(knifeTool)
            end)
        end

        local stabRemote = knifeTool:FindFirstChild("Stab") or knifeTool:FindFirstChild("Slash")
        local handle = knifeTool:FindFirstChild("Handle")
        local radius = tonumber(Values["kill_aura_radius"]) or 14

        for _, target in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if target ~= LocalPlayer and Ony.IsPlayerInActiveRound(target) and target.Character then
                local role = Ony.ResolvePlayerRole(target)
                local roleMatch = (not filterRole)
                    or (filterRole == "Sheriff" and (role == "Sheriff" or role == "Hero"))
                    or (filterRole == "Innocent" and role == "Innocent")

                local tHrp = target.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                local tHum = target.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if roleMatch and tHrp and tHum and tHum.Health > 0 then
                    local dist = (hrp.Position - tHrp.Position).Magnitude
                    local inRange = (filterRole ~= nil)
                        or (Flags["auto_kill_all"] == true)
                        or (Flags["kill_aura"] == true and dist <= radius)

                    if inRange then
                        pcall(function()
                            if stabRemote and stabRemote:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
                                stabRemote:FireServer("Down")
                            else
                                knifeTool:Activate()
                            end
                        end)

                        if handle and ExecutorCaps.HasFireTouchInterest then
                            pcall(function()
                                firetouchinterest(tHrp, handle, 0)
                                firetouchinterest(tHrp, handle, 1)
                            end)
                        else
                            pcall(function()
                                tHrp.CFrame = hrp.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, -2.2)
                            end)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end

    function Ony.ExecuteAutoThrowKnife()
        if not Flags["auto_throw_knife"] then
            return
        end
        if os.clock() - Ony.LastAutoKnifeThrowClock < 0.65 then
            return
        end

        local char = LocalPlayer.Character
        local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not char or not hrp or not hum then
            return
        end

        local hasKnife, knifeTool = Ony.HasKnifeTool(LocalPlayer)
        if not hasKnife or not knifeTool then
            return
        end

        local maxRange = tonumber(Values["auto_knife_range"]) or 150
        local requireVis = (Values["auto_knife_visible"] ~= false)

        local bestTarget = nil
        local bestPart = nil
        local bestDist = maxRange

        for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if plr ~= LocalPlayer and Ony.IsPlayerInActiveRound(plr) and plr.Character then
                local part = plr.Character:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
                    or plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if part then
                    local d = (hrp.Position - part.Position).Magnitude
                    if d <= bestDist and (not requireVis or MathEngine.IsPartVisible(part)) then
                        bestDist = d
                        bestTarget = plr
                        bestPart = part
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        if bestTarget and bestPart then
            Ony.LastAutoKnifeThrowClock = os.clock()
            if knifeTool.Parent ~= char then
                pcall(function()
                    hum:EquipTool(knifeTool)
                end)
                task.wait(0.05)
            end

            local predicted = MathEngine.PredictTargetPosition(bestPart, "Ping Prediction", 0.35)
            local throwRemote = knifeTool:FindFirstChild("Throw", true)
            if throwRemote and throwRemote:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
                pcall(function()
                    throwRemote:FireServer(CFrame.lookAt(hrp.Position, predicted), predicted)
                end)
                Ony.SpawnBulletImpactBeam(hrp.Position, predicted, Color3.fromRGB(251, 191, 36))
                if Flags["shot_info"] then
                    Ony.NotifyToast("Auto Knife Throw", "Threw Knife at " .. bestTarget.Name .. " (" .. math.floor(bestDist) .. " studs)")
                end
            end
        end
    end

    function Ony.ExecuteZombieInfectionLoop()
        local char = LocalPlayer.Character
        local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not char or not hrp then
            return
        end

        if Flags["auto_shoot_zombies"] then
            local hasGun, gunTool = Ony.HasGunTool(LocalPlayer)
            if hasGun and gunTool then
                for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                    if plr ~= LocalPlayer and Ony.IsPlayerInActiveRound(plr) and plr.Character then
                        local role = Ony.ResolvePlayerRole(plr)
                        local isZombie = (role == "Zombie" or role == "Murderer" or (plr.Team and string.find(string.lower(plr.Team.Name), "zombie")))
                        local tPart = plr.Character:FindFirstChild("Head") or plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                        if isZombie and tPart and MathEngine.IsPartVisible(tPart) then
                            local createBeam = gunTool:FindFirstChild("CreateBeam", true)
                            local rf = createBeam and createBeam:FindFirstChild("RemoteFunction")
                            if rf and rf:IsA("RemoteFunction") then
                                local pred = MathEngine.PredictTargetPosition(tPart, "Ping Prediction", 0)
                                pcall(function()
                                    rf:InvokeServer(1, pred, "AH2")
                                end)
                                break
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        if Flags["auto_infect_survivors"] then
            local infectRange = tonumber(Values["infect_range"]) or 20
            local hasKnife, knifeTool = Ony.HasKnifeTool(LocalPlayer)
            local handle = knifeTool and knifeTool:FindFirstChild("Handle")
            for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                if plr ~= LocalPlayer and Ony.IsPlayerInActiveRound(plr) and plr.Character then
                    local tHrp = plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    if tHrp and (hrp.Position - tHrp.Position).Magnitude <= infectRange then
                        if handle and ExecutorCaps.HasFireTouchInterest then
                            pcall(function()
                                firetouchinterest(tHrp, handle, 0)
                                firetouchinterest(tHrp, handle, 1)
                            end)
                        else
                            pcall(function()
                                tHrp.CFrame = hrp.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, -2)
                            end)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end

    function Ony.UpdateHitboxExpander()
        local enabled = (Flags["hitbox_expander"] == true)
        local sizeVal = tonumber(Values["hitbox_size"]) or 14
        local hitColor = (ItemColors["hitbox_expander"] and ItemColors["hitbox_expander"][1]) or Color3.fromRGB(52, 211, 153)

        if enabled then
            Ony.HitboxExpandedState = true
            for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                if plr ~= LocalPlayer and plr.Character then
                    local hrp = plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    if hrp and hrp:IsA("BasePart") then
                        pcall(function()
                            hrp.Size = Vector3.new(sizeVal, sizeVal, sizeVal)
                            hrp.Transparency = 0.68
                            hrp.Color = hitColor
                            hrp.Material = Enum.Material.Neon
                            hrp.CanCollide = false
                        end)
                    end
                end
            end
        elseif Ony.HitboxExpandedState then
            Ony.HitboxExpandedState = false
            for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                if plr ~= LocalPlayer and plr.Character then
                    local hrp = plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    if hrp and hrp:IsA("BasePart") then
                        pcall(function()
                            hrp.Size = Vector3.new(2, 2, 1)
                            hrp.Transparency = 1
                            hrp.Material = Enum.Material.Plastic
                            hrp.CanCollide = false
                        end)
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
end

    local function UpdateKeybindFlagsHUD()
        local showHud = (Flags["feature_flags_hud"] == true)
        KeybindFlagsHud.Visible = showHud
        if not showHud then
            return
        end

        local activeEntries = {}
        for _, tab in ipairs(Ony.UI_SCHEMA) do
            for _, grp in ipairs(tab.groups) do
                for _, item in ipairs(grp.items) do
                    if item.type == "checkbox" and Flags[item.id] then
                        local kb = ItemKeybinds[item.id]
                        local tag = kb and (" [" .. tostring(kb) .. "]") or " [ON]"
                        table.insert(activeEntries, item.label .. tag)
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        for i = 1, 14 do
            local slot = KeybindHudRows[i]
            local txt = activeEntries[i]
            if txt then
                slot.Frame.Visible = true
                slot.Label.Text = txt
                slot.Bar.BackgroundColor3 = Ony.AccentColor
            else
                slot.Frame.Visible = false
            end
        end
    end
    Ony.UpdateKeybindFlagsHUD = UpdateKeybindFlagsHUD

    Ony.CreatePlayerESPPack = CreatePlayerESPPack

    local function HidePlayerESPPack(pack)
        if not pack then
            return
        end
        pack.BoxFrame.Visible = false
        pack.HealthBg.Visible = false
        pack.NameLabel.Visible = false
        pack.WeaponLabel.Visible = false
        pack.FlagsLabel.Visible = false
        pack.TracerLine.Visible = false
        pack.ArrowEdge1.Visible = false
        pack.ArrowEdge2.Visible = false
        pack.ArrowEdge3.Visible = false
        pack.ExtrapFrame.Visible = false
        pack.ExtrapLine.Visible = false
        if pack.DrawingTriangle then
            pcall(function()
                pack.DrawingTriangle.Visible = false
            end)
        end
        for i = 1, 8 do
            pack.CornerLines[i].Visible = false
        end
        for i = 1, 12 do
            pack.Box3DLines[i].Visible = false
        end
        for i = 1, 14 do
            pack.SkeletonLines[i].Visible = false
        end
    end
    Ony.HidePlayerESPPack = HidePlayerESPPack

    local function DestroyPlayerESPPack(plr)
        local pack = PlayerESPRegistry[plr]
        if not pack then
            return
        end
        HidePlayerESPPack(pack)
        pcall(function() pack.BoxFrame:Destroy() end)
        pcall(function() pack.HealthBg:Destroy() end)
        pcall(function() pack.NameLabel:Destroy() end)
        pcall(function() pack.WeaponLabel:Destroy() end)
        pcall(function() pack.FlagsLabel:Destroy() end)
        pcall(function() pack.TracerLine:Destroy() end)
        pcall(function() pack.ArrowEdge1:Destroy() end)
        pcall(function() pack.ArrowEdge2:Destroy() end)
        pcall(function() pack.ArrowEdge3:Destroy() end)
        pcall(function() pack.ExtrapFrame:Destroy() end)
        pcall(function() pack.ExtrapLine:Destroy() end)
        if pack.DrawingTriangle then
            pcall(function() pack.DrawingTriangle:Remove() end)
        end
        for i = 1, 8 do
            pcall(function() pack.CornerLines[i]:Destroy() end)
        end
        for i = 1, 12 do
            pcall(function() pack.Box3DLines[i]:Destroy() end)
        end
        for i = 1, 14 do
            pcall(function() pack.SkeletonLines[i]:Destroy() end)
        end
        PlayerESPRegistry[plr] = nil
    end

    Ony.TrackConnection(Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(DestroyPlayerESPPack))

    -- // 6.4 World Items ESP Pool (Dropped Gun, CoinContainer Coins, Murderer Traps, Sound ESP Rings)
