        TrapEspLabelsPool[i] = tl
    end

    -- // Knife Equip & Slash Sound ESP Detector
    local LastSoundAlertClock = 0
    local LastSoundWorldPos = nil

    local function CheckCharacterKnifeSounds()
        if not Flags["sound_esp"] then
            LastSoundWorldPos = nil
            return
        end
        for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if plr ~= LocalPlayer and plr.Character then
                for _, desc in ipairs(plr.Character:GetDescendants()) do
                    if desc:IsA("Sound") and desc.IsPlaying then
                        local sName = string.lower(tostring(desc.Name))
                        if string.find(sName, "slash") or string.find(sName, "stab") or string.find(sName, "equip") or string.find(sName, "throw") or (desc.Parent and desc.Parent.Parent and desc.Parent.Parent.Name == "Knife") then
                            local hrp = plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                            if hrp then
                                LastSoundWorldPos = hrp.Position
                                if os.clock() - LastSoundAlertClock > 2.2 then
                                    LastSoundAlertClock = os.clock()
                                    NotifyToast("Sound ESP Alert", "Knife audio detected from " .. plr.Name .. "!", Color3.fromRGB(255, 42, 75))
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    Ony.CheckCharacterKnifeSounds = CheckCharacterKnifeSounds

    local SoundEspRing = Instance.new("Frame")
    SoundEspRing.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
    SoundEspRing.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(32, 32)
    SoundEspRing.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    SoundEspRing.Visible = false
    SoundEspRing.ZIndex = 7
    SoundEspRing.Parent = VectorOverlay
    Instance.new("UICorner", SoundEspRing).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
    local SoundEspStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", SoundEspRing)
    SoundEspStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 42, 75)
    SoundEspStroke.Thickness = 2

    -- // GunDrop Spawn Notification Listener
    local LastNotifiedGunDropInstance = nil

    local function UpdateWorldItemsESP(vpCenter, dynamicColor)
        local gunDrop = Workspace:FindFirstChild("GunDrop", true)
        if gunDrop and gunDrop:IsA("BasePart") then
            local dist = math.floor(MathEngine.GetDistanceFromLocal(gunDrop.Position))
            Telemetry.GunDropped = true
            Telemetry.GunDropDistance = dist

            if Flags["notify_gun_drop"] and LastNotifiedGunDropInstance ~= gunDrop then
                LastNotifiedGunDropInstance = gunDrop
                PlayFeedbackBeep(1.3)
                NotifyToast("Gun Drop Alert!", "Sheriff Gun dropped (" .. tostring(dist) .. " studs away)!", Color3.fromRGB(255, 213, 42))
            end

            -- 3D Highlight on GunDrop
            local gdHighlight = gunDrop:FindFirstChild("OnyGunDropHighlight")
            if Flags["dropped_gun_esp"] then
                if not gdHighlight then
                    gdHighlight = Instance.new("Highlight")
                    gdHighlight.Name = "OnyGunDropHighlight"
                    gdHighlight.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop
                    gdHighlight.FillColor = (ItemColors["dropped_gun_esp"] and ItemColors["dropped_gun_esp"][1]) or Color3.fromRGB(255, 213, 42)
                    gdHighlight.OutlineColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
                    gdHighlight.FillTransparency = 0.25
                    gdHighlight.Parent = gunDrop
                end
                local sPos, onScr = MathEngine.WorldToScreen(gunDrop.Position)
                if onScr then
                    DroppedGunBillboard.Visible = true
                    DroppedGunBillboard.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(sPos.X, sPos.Y - 18)
                    DroppedGunBillboard.Text = string.format("[GUNDROP | %d STUDS]", dist)
                    SetGuiLine(
                        DroppedGunTracer,
                        Vector2.new(vpCenter.X, Camera.ViewportSize.Y - 20),
                        sPos,
                        1.5,
                        Color3.fromRGB(255, 213, 42)
                    )
                else
                    DroppedGunBillboard.Visible = false
                    DroppedGunTracer.Visible = false
                end
            else
                if gdHighlight then
                    gdHighlight:Destroy()
                end
                DroppedGunBillboard.Visible = false
                DroppedGunTracer.Visible = false
            end
        else
            Telemetry.GunDropped = false
            Telemetry.GunDropDistance = 0
            DroppedGunBillboard.Visible = false
            DroppedGunTracer.Visible = false
        end

        -- Gun Drop Top State Banner
        if Flags["gun_drop_state"] then
            GunDropBanner.Visible = true
            if Telemetry.GunDropped then
                GunDropBannerText.Text = string.format("GUNDROP STATE: DROPPED ON GROUND (%d STUDS)", Telemetry.GunDropDistance)
                GunDropBannerText.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 213, 42)
                GunDropBannerStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 213, 42)
            elseif Telemetry.HeroName ~= "None" then
                GunDropBannerText.Text = "GUNDROP STATE: CLAIMED BY HERO (" .. Telemetry.HeroName .. ")"
                GunDropBannerText.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(42, 255, 101)
                GunDropBannerStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(42, 255, 101)
            else
                GunDropBannerText.Text = "GUNDROP STATE: HELD BY SHERIFF (" .. Telemetry.SheriffName .. ")"
                GunDropBannerText.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(56, 189, 248)
                GunDropBannerStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(56, 189, 248)
            end
        else
            GunDropBanner.Visible = false
        end

        -- CoinContainer ESP Dots
        if Flags["coin_esp"] then
            local map = FindActiveMap()
            local container = map and map:FindFirstChild("CoinContainer")
            local coinCol = (ItemColors["coin_esp"] and ItemColors["coin_esp"][1]) or Color3.fromRGB(251, 191, 36)
            local usedIdx = 0
            if container then
                for _, coin in ipairs(container:GetChildren()) do
                    if usedIdx >= 24 then
                        break
                    end
                    local part = coin:IsA("BasePart") and coin or coin:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")
                    if part and part.Transparency < 0.9 then
                        local sp, onScr = MathEngine.WorldToScreen(part.Position)
                        if onScr then
                            usedIdx = usedIdx + 1
                            local dot = CoinEspDotsPool[usedIdx]
                            dot.Visible = true
                            dot.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(sp.X, sp.Y)
                            dot.BackgroundColor3 = coinCol
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
            for i = usedIdx + 1, 24 do
                CoinEspDotsPool[i].Visible = false
            end
        else
            for i = 1, 24 do
                CoinEspDotsPool[i].Visible = false
            end
        end

        -- Murderer Trap ESP
        if Flags["trap_esp"] then
            local trapCol = (ItemColors["trap_esp"] and ItemColors["trap_esp"][1]) or Color3.fromRGB(239, 68, 68)
            local tIdx = 0
            for _, obj in ipairs(Workspace:GetDescendants()) do
                if tIdx >= 8 then
                    break
                end
                if obj.Name == "Trap" and (obj:IsA("BasePart") or obj:IsA("Model")) then
                    local pos = obj:IsA("BasePart") and obj.Position or (obj:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart") and obj:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart").Position)
                    if pos then
                        local sp, onScr = MathEngine.WorldToScreen(pos)
                        if onScr then
                            tIdx = tIdx + 1
                            local tl = TrapEspLabelsPool[tIdx]
                            tl.Visible = true
                            tl.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(sp.X, sp.Y)
                            tl.TextColor3 = trapCol
                            tl.Text = string.format("[MURDERER TRAP | %dm]", math.floor(MathEngine.GetDistanceFromLocal(pos)))
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
            for i = tIdx + 1, 8 do
                TrapEspLabelsPool[i].Visible = false
            end
        else
            for i = 1, 8 do
                TrapEspLabelsPool[i].Visible = false
            end
        end

        -- Sound ESP Indicator Ring
        if Flags["sound_esp"] and LastSoundWorldPos and (os.clock() - LastSoundAlertClock < 2.2) then
            local sp, onScr = MathEngine.WorldToScreen(LastSoundWorldPos)
            if onScr then
                SoundEspRing.Visible = true
                local pulse = 24 + math.sin(os.clock() * 14) * 10
                SoundEspRing.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(sp.X, sp.Y)
                SoundEspRing.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(pulse, pulse)
            else
                SoundEspRing.Visible = false
            end
        else
            SoundEspRing.Visible = false
        end
    end
    Ony.UpdateWorldItemsESP = UpdateWorldItemsESP

    -- // 6.5 Overhead 3D BillboardGui Head Tags Manager
    local function UpdatePlayerBillboardHeadTag(plr, char, role, roleColor)
        local head = char and char:FindFirstChild("Head")
        if not head then
            return
        end
        local existing = head:FindFirstChild("OnyHeadTagBillboard")
        local shouldShow = Flags["flags_esp"] and (Values["enable_head_tags"] ~= false)

        if shouldShow then
            if not existing then
                existing = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
                existing.Name = "OnyHeadTagBillboard"
                existing.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(140, 26)
                existing.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 2.6, 0)
                existing.AlwaysOnTop = true
                existing.Parent = head

                local badge = Instance.new("Frame")
                badge.Name = "Badge"
                badge.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
                badge.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(16, 13, 24)
                badge.BackgroundTransparency = 0.2
                badge.BorderSizePixel = 0
                badge.Parent = existing
                Instance.new("UICorner", badge).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 5)

                local st = Instance.new("UIStroke", badge)
                st.Name = "Stroke"
                st.Thickness = 1.2
                st.Color = roleColor

                local txt = Instance.new("TextLabel")
                txt.Name = "TagText"
                txt.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
                txt.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                txt.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
                txt.TextSize = 11
                txt.TextColor3 = roleColor
                txt.Parent = badge
            end
            local badge = existing:FindFirstChild("Badge")
            if badge then
                local st = badge:FindFirstChild("Stroke")
                local txt = badge:FindFirstChild("TagText")
                if st then st.Color = roleColor end
                if txt then
                    txt.TextColor3 = roleColor
                    txt.Text = string.upper(role) .. " • " .. plr.Name
                end
            end
        elseif existing then
            existing:Destroy()
        end
    end
    Ony.UpdatePlayerBillboardHeadTag = UpdatePlayerBillboardHeadTag

    -- // 6.6 Streamer Mode (Hide My Name) & Lighting / Ambience Controller
    local StreamerModeApplied = false
    local AmbienceEffectInstance = nil
    local FpsBoostApplied = false

    local function UpdateStreamerModeAndAmbience()
        -- Streamer Mode (hide_my_name)
        if Flags["hide_my_name"] then
            StreamerModeApplied = true
            local char = LocalPlayer.Character
            local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if hum then
                hum.DisplayName = "Ony.wtf_User"
            end
        elseif StreamerModeApplied then
            StreamerModeApplied = false
            local char = LocalPlayer.Character
            local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if hum then
                hum.DisplayName = Ony.OriginalDefaults.DisplayName
            end
        end

        -- Screen Ambience Tint (screen_ambience)
        if Flags["screen_ambience"] then
            local tintCol = (ItemColors["screen_ambience"] and ItemColors["screen_ambience"][1]) or Color3.fromRGB(100, 22, 175)
            if not AmbienceEffectInstance or not AmbienceEffectInstance.Parent then
                AmbienceEffectInstance = Instance.new("ColorCorrectionEffect")
                AmbienceEffectInstance.Name = "OnyScreenAmbience"
                AmbienceEffectInstance.Parent = Lighting
                Ony.TrackWorldInstance(AmbienceEffectInstance)
            end
            AmbienceEffectInstance.TintColor = tintCol:Lerp(Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255), 0.55)
            AmbienceEffectInstance.Saturation = 0.25
            AmbienceEffectInstance.Contrast = 0.1
        elseif AmbienceEffectInstance then
            pcall(function()
                AmbienceEffectInstance:Destroy()
            end)
            AmbienceEffectInstance = nil
        end

        -- Boost FPS & Full Brightness (boost_fps)
        if Flags["boost_fps"] then
            FpsBoostApplied = true
            Lighting.Brightness = 2.5
            Lighting.ClockTime = 14
            Lighting.FogEnd = 100000
            Lighting.GlobalShadows = false
            Lighting.Ambient = Color3.fromRGB(185, 185, 195)
            Lighting.OutdoorAmbient = Color3.fromRGB(185, 185, 195)
        elseif FpsBoostApplied then
            FpsBoostApplied = false
            Lighting.Brightness = Ony.OriginalLightingState.Brightness
            Lighting.ClockTime = Ony.OriginalLightingState.ClockTime
            Lighting.FogEnd = Ony.OriginalLightingState.FogEnd
            Lighting.GlobalShadows = Ony.OriginalLightingState.GlobalShadows
            Lighting.Ambient = Ony.OriginalLightingState.Ambient
            Lighting.OutdoorAmbient = Ony.OriginalLightingState.OutdoorAmbient
        end
    end
    Ony.UpdateStreamerModeAndAmbience = UpdateStreamerModeAndAmbience
end


-- ============================================================================
-- SECTION 7: WORLD, AUTOFARM, SURVIVAL, MOVEMENT, PLAYERS & MISC SUBSYSTEMS
-- ============================================================================
do
    local NotifyToast = Ony.NotifyToast
    local FindActiveMap = Ony.FindActiveMap
    local FindBestCoinTarget = Ony.FindBestCoinTarget
    local GetMurdererPlayer = Ony.GetMurdererPlayer
    local GetSheriffPlayer = Ony.GetSheriffPlayer
    local GetClosestAlivePlayerToPoint = Ony.GetClosestAlivePlayerToPoint
    local IsPlayerInActiveRound = Ony.IsPlayerInActiveRound
    local ResolvePlayerRole = Ony.ResolvePlayerRole
    local RefreshMM2Roles = Ony.RefreshMM2Roles
    local ShootMurdererWithGun = Ony.ShootMurdererWithGun
    local ExecuteKnifeCombat = Ony.ExecuteKnifeCombat
    local LocateMM2Remotes = Ony.LocateMM2Remotes

    -- // 7.1 Elevated Safezone Sky Platform & Air Walk Platform
    local SafezonePlatformPart = nil
    local AirWalkPlatformPart = nil
    local CurrentAirWalkY = nil

    local function GetOrCreateSafezonePlatform()
        if SafezonePlatformPart and SafezonePlatformPart.Parent == Workspace then
            return SafezonePlatformPart
        end
        local part = Instance.new("Part")
        part.Name = "OnySafezoneSkyPlatform"
        part.Size = Vector3.new(85, 2, 85)
        part.Anchored = true
        part.CanCollide = true
        part.Material = Enum.Material.ForceField
        part.Color = Ony.AccentColor
        part.Transparency = 0.45
        part.Position = Vector3.new(0, 260, 0)
        part.Parent = Workspace
        Ony.TrackWorldInstance(part)
        SafezonePlatformPart = part
        return part
    end

    local function TeleportToSafezonePlatform()
        local char = LocalPlayer.Character
        local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not hrp then
            return
        end
        local map = FindActiveMap()
        local basePos = hrp.Position
        if map and map:FindFirstChild("Spawns") then
            local sp = map.Spawns:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")
            if sp then
                basePos = sp.Position
            end
        end
        local plat = GetOrCreateSafezonePlatform()
        plat.Position = Vector3.new(basePos.X, basePos.Y + 135, basePos.Z)
        hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
        hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(plat.Position + Vector3.new(0, 4.5, 0))
    end
    Ony.TeleportToSafezonePlatform = TeleportToSafezonePlatform

    local function UpdateAirWalkPlatform()
        local char = LocalPlayer.Character
        local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

        if Flags["air_walk"] and hrp and hum then
            if not AirWalkPlatformPart or not AirWalkPlatformPart.Parent then
                AirWalkPlatformPart = Instance.new("Part")
                AirWalkPlatformPart.Name = "OnyAirWalkPad"
                AirWalkPlatformPart.Size = Vector3.new(9, 0.6, 9)
                AirWalkPlatformPart.Anchored = true
                AirWalkPlatformPart.CanCollide = true
                AirWalkPlatformPart.Material = Enum.Material.Neon
                AirWalkPlatformPart.Color = Ony.AccentColor
                AirWalkPlatformPart.Transparency = 0.65
                AirWalkPlatformPart.Parent = Workspace
                Ony.TrackWorldInstance(AirWalkPlatformPart)
            end
            if CurrentAirWalkY == nil then
                CurrentAirWalkY = hrp.Position.Y - 3.3
            end
            if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Space) then
                CurrentAirWalkY = CurrentAirWalkY + 0.35
            elseif UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.LeftControl) or UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.LeftShift) then
                CurrentAirWalkY = CurrentAirWalkY - 0.35
            end
            AirWalkPlatformPart.Position = Vector3.new(hrp.Position.X, CurrentAirWalkY, hrp.Position.Z)
        else
            CurrentAirWalkY = nil
            if AirWalkPlatformPart then
                pcall(function()
                    AirWalkPlatformPart:Destroy()
                end)
                AirWalkPlatformPart = nil
            end
        end
    end
    Ony.UpdateAirWalkPlatform = UpdateAirWalkPlatform

    -- // 7.2 Dropped Gun Auto / Manual Grabber
    local IsGrabbingGunLock = false

    local function GrabDroppedGun(notifyUser)
        if IsGrabbingGunLock then
            return
        end
        local gunDrop = Workspace:FindFirstChild("GunDrop", true)
        if not gunDrop or not gunDrop:IsA("BasePart") then
            if notifyUser then
                NotifyToast("Ony.wtf Gun Grab", "No GunDrop currently on the map!")
            end
            return
        end
        local char = LocalPlayer.Character
        local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not hrp then
            return
        end

        IsGrabbingGunLock = true
        local savedCf = hrp.CFrame

        pcall(function()
            if ExecutorCaps.HasFireTouchInterest then
                firetouchinterest(hrp, gunDrop, 0)
                firetouchinterest(hrp, gunDrop, 1)
            end
            hrp.CFrame = gunDrop.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 1.5, 0)
            task.wait(0.11)
            if hrp and hrp.Parent then
                hrp.CFrame = savedCf
            end
        end)

        if notifyUser then
            NotifyToast("Ony.wtf Gun Grab", "Claimed Dropped Gun & restored original position!", Color3.fromRGB(255, 213, 42))
        end
        task.delay(0.35, function()
            IsGrabbingGunLock = false
        end)
    end
    Ony.GrabDroppedGun = GrabDroppedGun

    -- // 7.3 Innocent Survival Suite (Survive Safezone, Avoid Murderer Kite, Fake Dead Ragdoll)
    local FakeDeadActiveState = false

    local function UpdateInnocentSurvivalSuite()
        local char = LocalPlayer.Character
        local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not char or not hrp or not hum then
            return
        end

        -- Survive on Safezone
        if Flags["survive_safezone"] then
            local plat = GetOrCreateSafezonePlatform()
            if (hrp.Position - plat.Position).Magnitude > 42 then
                TeleportToSafezonePlatform()
            end
        end

        -- Avoid Murderer (Auto Kite Vector Escape)
        if Flags["avoid_murderer"] then
            local murderer = GetMurdererPlayer()
            local mHrp = murderer and murderer.Character and murderer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if mHrp then
                local safeDist = tonumber(Values["avoid_distance"]) or 35
                local delta = hrp.Position - mHrp.Position
                local currDist = delta.Magnitude
                if currDist < safeDist and currDist > 0.1 then
                    local escapeDir = Vector3.new(delta.X, 0, delta.Z).Unit
                    local targetEscapePos = hrp.Position + escapeDir * (safeDist - currDist + 14)
                    hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(targetEscapePos, targetEscapePos + escapeDir)
                end
            end
        end

        -- Fake Dead (Lay Prone / Ragdoll Pose)
        if Flags["fake_dead"] then
            FakeDeadActiveState = true
            hum.PlatformStand = true
            hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(hrp.Position) * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(90), 0, 0)
        elseif FakeDeadActiveState then
            FakeDeadActiveState = false
            hum.PlatformStand = false
            hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(hrp.Position + Vector3.new(0, 2.5, 0))
        end
    end
    Ony.UpdateInnocentSurvivalSuite = UpdateInnocentSurvivalSuite

    -- // 7.4 CoinContainer Tween Autofarm & Bag Full Handlers
    local ActiveFarmTween = nil
    local BagFullHandledThisRound = false

    local function StopActiveFarmTween()
        if ActiveFarmTween then
            pcall(function()
                ActiveFarmTween:Cancel()
            end)
            ActiveFarmTween = nil
        end
    end
    Ony.StopActiveFarmTween = StopActiveFarmTween

    local function ExecuteAutofarmStep()
        if not Flags["auto_farm"] then
            StopActiveFarmTween()
            return
        end

        local char = LocalPlayer.Character
        local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not char or not hrp or not hum or hum.Health <= 0 then
            return
        end

        -- Check if Coin Bag is Full (40 or 50)
        if Telemetry.CoinsCollected >= Telemetry.BagCapacity then
            StopActiveFarmTween()
            if not BagFullHandledThisRound then
                BagFullHandledThisRound = true
                NotifyToast("Coin Bag Full!", string.format("Collected %d/%d coins!", Telemetry.CoinsCollected, Telemetry.BagCapacity), Color3.fromRGB(42, 255, 101))

                if Flags["webhook_bag_full"] and Ony.SendWebhookEvent then
                    Ony.SendWebhookEvent("MM2 Coin Bag Full", string.format("Bag reached **%d/%d** coins in map **%s**.", Telemetry.CoinsCollected, Telemetry.BagCapacity, tostring(Telemetry.ActiveMapName)))
                end

                if Flags["auto_reset_bag_full"] then
                    hum.Health = 0
                elseif Flags["auto_safezone_bag_full"] then
                    TeleportToSafezonePlatform()
                elseif Flags["auto_win_bag_full"] then
                    local myRole = ResolvePlayerRole(LocalPlayer)
                    if myRole == "Sheriff" or myRole == "Hero" then
                        ShootMurdererWithGun(false, true)
                    elseif myRole == "Murderer" then
                        ExecuteKnifeCombat(nil)
                    else
                        TeleportToSafezonePlatform()
                    end
                end
            end
            return
        else
            BagFullHandledThisRound = false
        end

        -- Auto Claim Battlepass / Event Rewards if toggled
        if Flags["auto_claim_rewards"] then
            pcall(function()
                local claimRemote = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("ClaimReward", true)
                    or ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("ClaimPassReward", true)
                if claimRemote and claimRemote:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
                    claimRemote:FireServer()
                end
            end)
        end

        local bestCoin, dist = FindBestCoinTarget()
        if not bestCoin then
            return
        end

        local farmSpeed = MathEngine.Clamp(tonumber(Values["farm_speed"]) or 22, 10, 45)
        local duration = MathEngine.Clamp(dist / farmSpeed, 0.12, 4.5)

        local targetCf
        if Flags["underground_farm"] then
            hum.PlatformStand = true
            targetCf = CFrame.new(bestCoin.Position - Vector3.new(0, 3.2, 0)) * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(90), 0, 0)
        else
            if not Flags["fake_dead"] then
                hum.PlatformStand = false
            end
            targetCf = CFrame.new(bestCoin.Position)
        end

        hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
        hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero

        StopActiveFarmTween()
        ActiveFarmTween = TweenService:Create(
            hrp,
            TweenInfo.new(duration, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear, Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
            { CFrame = targetCf }
        )
        ActiveFarmTween:Play()

        if dist <= 9.5 then
            if ExecutorCaps.HasFireTouchInterest then
                pcall(function()
                    firetouchinterest(hrp, bestCoin, 0)
                    firetouchinterest(hrp, bestCoin, 1)
                end)
            end
            Telemetry.CoinsCollected = math.min(Telemetry.BagCapacity, Telemetry.CoinsCollected + 1)
        end
    end
    Ony.ExecuteAutofarmStep = ExecuteAutofarmStep

    -- // 7.5 Classic 3D Omnidirectional 6-DOF Flight Engine (Full Pitch/Vertical + BodyMover & CFrame Hybrid)
    local FlyBodyVelocity = nil
    local FlyBodyGyro = nil
    local FlyPlatformStandActive = false

    local function UpdateFlightController(dt)
        local stepDt = tonumber(dt) or 0.016
        local char = LocalPlayer.Character
        local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

        if Flags["fly_mode"] and hrp and hum and Camera then
            FlyPlatformStandActive = true
            hum.PlatformStand = true

            if not FlyBodyVelocity or FlyBodyVelocity.Parent ~= hrp then
                if FlyBodyVelocity then pcall(function() FlyBodyVelocity:Destroy() end) end
                FlyBodyVelocity = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
                FlyBodyVelocity.Name = "OnyFlyVelocity"
                FlyBodyVelocity.MaxForce = Vector3.new(9e8, 9e8, 9e8)
                FlyBodyVelocity.P = 12500
                FlyBodyVelocity.Velocity = Vector3.zero
                FlyBodyVelocity.Parent = hrp
            end
            if not FlyBodyGyro or FlyBodyGyro.Parent ~= hrp then
                if FlyBodyGyro then pcall(function() FlyBodyGyro:Destroy() end) end
                FlyBodyGyro = Instance.new("BodyGyro")
                FlyBodyGyro.Name = "OnyFlyGyro"
                FlyBodyGyro.MaxTorque = Vector3.new(9e8, 9e8, 9e8)
                FlyBodyGyro.P = 12500
                FlyBodyGyro.D = 450
                FlyBodyGyro.CFrame = Camera.CFrame
                FlyBodyGyro.Parent = hrp
            end

            local flySpeed = tonumber(Values["fly_speed"]) or 50
            local moveDir = Vector3.zero
            local camCf = Camera.CFrame
            local lookVec = camCf.LookVector
            local rightVec = camCf.RightVector

            -- Classic 3D Camera-Directional WASD (includes vertical pitch up/down when looking up/down)
            if not UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() then
                if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.W) or UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Up) then
                    moveDir = moveDir + lookVec
                end
                if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.S) or UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Down) then
                    moveDir = moveDir - lookVec
                end
                if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.D) or UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Right) then
                    moveDir = moveDir + rightVec
                end
                if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.A) or UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Left) then
                    moveDir = moveDir - rightVec
                end
                -- Dedicated Vertical Ascent / Descent Keys (Space/E = Up, LeftShift/LeftControl/C = Down)
                if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Space) or UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.E) then
                    moveDir = moveDir + Vector3.new(0, 1, 0)
                end
                if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.LeftShift) or UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.LeftControl) or UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.C) then
                    moveDir = moveDir - Vector3.new(0, 1, 0)
                end
            end

            -- Mobile / Gamepad Thumbstick 3D Camera Projection Fallback
            if moveDir.Magnitude == 0 and hum.MoveDirection.Magnitude > 0 then
                local flatLook = Vector3.new(lookVec.X, 0, lookVec.Z)
                local flatRight = Vector3.new(rightVec.X, 0, rightVec.Z)
                if flatLook.Magnitude > 0.01 and flatRight.Magnitude > 0.01 then
                    local fUnit = flatLook.Unit
                    local rUnit = flatRight.Unit
                    local forwardAmount = hum.MoveDirection:Dot(fUnit)
                    local rightAmount = hum.MoveDirection:Dot(rUnit)
                    moveDir = (lookVec * forwardAmount) + (rightVec * rightAmount)
                else
                    moveDir = hum.MoveDirection
                end
            end

            if moveDir.Magnitude > 0.001 then
                local unitVel = moveDir.Unit * flySpeed
                FlyBodyVelocity.Velocity = unitVel
                hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = unitVel
                hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(hrp.Position + (unitVel * stepDt)) * (camCf - camCf.Position)
            else
                FlyBodyVelocity.Velocity = Vector3.zero
                hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(hrp.Position) * (camCf - camCf.Position)
            end
            FlyBodyGyro.CFrame = camCf
        else
            if FlyBodyVelocity then
                pcall(function() FlyBodyVelocity:Destroy() end)
                FlyBodyVelocity = nil
            end
            if FlyBodyGyro then
                pcall(function() FlyBodyGyro:Destroy() end)
                FlyBodyGyro = nil
            end
            if FlyPlatformStandActive then
                FlyPlatformStandActive = false
                if hum then
                    hum.PlatformStand = false
                    pcall(function()
                        hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp)
                    end)
                end
                if hrp then
                    hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                    hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                end
            end
        end
    end
    Ony.UpdateFlightController = UpdateFlightController

    -- // 7.6 Seamless Safezone + Controllable Clone Invisibility Engine
    -- Moves the real character safely to the Elevated Safezone Sky Platform while giving
    -- the player 100% responsive control of a local clone at their current location.
    -- When disabled, seamlessly transfers the real character to the clone's exact CFrame,
    -- velocity, camera angle, and movement vector in a single frame with zero teleport snap!
    local InvisRealCharacter = nil
    local InvisCloneCharacter = nil
    local InvisCloneActive = false

    local function DisableInvisibilityCloneSeamless(forceFallback)
        if not InvisCloneActive then
            return
        end
        InvisCloneActive = false

        local realChar = InvisRealCharacter
        local cloneChar = InvisCloneCharacter
        InvisRealCharacter = nil
        InvisCloneCharacter = nil

        local realHrp = realChar and realChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local realHum = realChar and realChar:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        local cloneHrp = cloneChar and cloneChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local cloneHum = cloneChar and cloneChar:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

        local savedCamCf = Camera and Camera.CFrame
        local targetCf = cloneHrp and cloneHrp.CFrame or (realHrp and realHrp.CFrame)
        local targetVel = cloneHrp and cloneHrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity or Vector3.zero
        local targetRotVel = cloneHrp and cloneHrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity or Vector3.zero
        local targetMoveDir = cloneHum and cloneHum.MoveDirection or Vector3.zero
        local targetState = cloneHum and cloneHum:GetState() or Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running

        if realChar and realChar.Parent and realHrp and realHum then
            pcall(function()
                -- Transfer any equipped tool from clone back to real character
                if cloneChar then
                    for _, child in ipairs(cloneChar:GetChildren()) do
                        if child:IsA("Tool") then
                            child.Parent = realChar
                        end
                    end
                end

                realHrp.Anchored = false
                for _, desc in ipairs(realChar:GetDescendants()) do
                    if desc:IsA("BasePart") or desc:IsA("Decal") then
                        desc.LocalTransparencyModifier = 0
                    end
                end

                if targetCf and not forceFallback then
                    realHrp.CFrame = targetCf
                    realHrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = targetVel
                    realHrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = targetRotVel
                end

                LocalPlayer.Character = realChar
                if Camera then
                    Camera.CameraSubject = realHum
                    if savedCamCf then
                        Camera.CFrame = savedCamCf
                    end
                end

                if targetState == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping or targetState == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Freefall then
                    realHum:ChangeState(targetState)
                else
                    realHum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running)
                end
                if targetMoveDir.Magnitude > 0 then
                    realHum:Move(targetMoveDir, false)
                end
            end)
        end

        if cloneChar then
            pcall(function()
                cloneChar:Destroy()
            end)
        end
    end
    Ony.DisableInvisibilityCloneSeamless = DisableInvisibilityCloneSeamless

    local function EnableInvisibilityCloneSeamless()
        if InvisCloneActive then
            return
        end
        local realChar = LocalPlayer.Character
        local realHrp = realChar and realChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local realHum = realChar and realChar:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not realChar or not realHrp or not realHum or realHum.Health <= 0 then
            return
        end

        local startCf = realHrp.CFrame
        local startVel = realHrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity
        local startMoveDir = realHum.MoveDirection
        local savedCamCf = Camera and Camera.CFrame

        local ok, cloneChar = pcall(function()
            realChar.Archivable = true
            local c = realChar:Clone()
            c.Name = "OnyInvisControllerClone"
            return c
        end)

        local cloneHrp = ok and cloneChar and cloneChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local cloneHum = ok and cloneChar and cloneChar:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not cloneHrp or not cloneHum then
            if cloneChar then pcall(function() cloneChar:Destroy() end) end
            return
        end

        InvisRealCharacter = realChar
        InvisCloneCharacter = cloneChar
        InvisCloneActive = true

        pcall(function()
            -- 1. Place Controllable Clone at Exact Current Position & Velocity
            cloneChar.Parent = Workspace
            cloneHrp.Anchored = false
            cloneHrp.CFrame = startCf
            cloneHrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = startVel
            Ony.TrackWorldInstance(cloneChar)

            -- Style Clone with Subtle Ghost Shimmer on Local Screen
            for _, desc in ipairs(cloneChar:GetDescendants()) do
                if (desc:IsA("BasePart") or desc:IsA("Decal")) and desc.Name ~= "HumanoidRootPart" then
                    desc.LocalTransparencyModifier = 0.32
                end
            end

            -- 2. Send Real Character to Elevated Safezone Sky Platform & Anchor Safely
            local plat = GetOrCreateSafezonePlatform()
            local map = FindActiveMap()
            local basePos = startCf.Position
            if map and map:FindFirstChild("Spawns") then
                local sp = map.Spawns:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")
                if sp then basePos = sp.Position end
            end
            plat.Position = Vector3.new(basePos.X, basePos.Y + 145, basePos.Z)
            realHrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
            realHrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
            realHrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(plat.Position + Vector3.new(0, 4.5, 0))
            realHrp.Anchored = true

            -- 3. Seamlessly Switch LocalPlayer Control & Camera to Clone Without Frame Jerk
            LocalPlayer.Character = cloneChar
            if Camera then
                Camera.CameraSubject = cloneHum
                if savedCamCf then
                    Camera.CFrame = savedCamCf
                end
            end
            if startMoveDir.Magnitude > 0 then
                cloneHum:Move(startMoveDir, false)
            end
        end)

        NotifyToast("Clone Invisibility", "Real character anchored on Safezone! Controlling ghost clone.")
    end

    local function UpdateInvisibilityState()
        if Flags["invisibility"] then
            if not InvisCloneActive then
                EnableInvisibilityCloneSeamless()
            else
                -- Verify both real character and clone character remain valid
                local realValid = InvisRealCharacter
                    and InvisRealCharacter.Parent
                    and InvisRealCharacter:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    and InvisRealCharacter:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                    and InvisRealCharacter:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid").Health > 0
                local cloneValid = InvisCloneCharacter
                    and InvisCloneCharacter.Parent
                    and InvisCloneCharacter:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    and InvisCloneCharacter:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                    and InvisCloneCharacter:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid").Health > 0

                if not realValid or not cloneValid then
                    DisableInvisibilityCloneSeamless(true)
                    Ony.SetFlagState("invisibility", false, true)
                else
                    -- Keep Real Character Safely Anchored on Sky Platform
                    local realHrp = InvisRealCharacter:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    if realHrp and SafezonePlatformPart then
                        realHrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                        realHrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(SafezonePlatformPart.Position + Vector3.new(0, 4.5, 0))
                        realHrp.Anchored = true
                    end
                    -- Ensure LocalTransparencyModifier stays visible as a sleek ghost on Clone
                    for _, desc in ipairs(InvisCloneCharacter:GetDescendants()) do
                        if (desc:IsA("BasePart") or desc:IsA("Decal")) and desc.Name ~= "HumanoidRootPart" then
                            desc.LocalTransparencyModifier = 0.32
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        elseif InvisCloneActive then
            DisableInvisibilityCloneSeamless(false)
            NotifyToast("Clone Invisibility", "Synced back to clone position seamlessly!")
        end
    end
    Ony.UpdateInvisibilityState = UpdateInvisibilityState

    local function ApplyAnimationPack(packId)
        local char = LocalPlayer.Character
        local animate = char and char:FindFirstChild("Animate")
        local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not animate or not hum then
            NotifyToast("Animation Pack", "Spawn into R15 character first!")
            return
        end
        local pack = R15_ANIM_PACKS[packId]
        if not pack then
            return
        end
        pcall(function()
            animate.Disabled = true
            if animate:FindFirstChild("idle") then
                if animate.idle:FindFirstChild("Animation1") then
                    animate.idle.Animation1.AnimationId = "rbxassetid://" .. pack.idle1
                end
                if animate.idle:FindFirstChild("Animation2") then
                    animate.idle.Animation2.AnimationId = "rbxassetid://" .. pack.idle2
                end
            end
            if animate:FindFirstChild("walk") and animate.walk:FindFirstChild("WalkAnim") then
                animate.walk.WalkAnim.AnimationId = "rbxassetid://" .. pack.walk
            end
            if animate:FindFirstChild("run") and animate.run:FindFirstChild("RunAnim") then
                animate.run.RunAnim.AnimationId = "rbxassetid://" .. pack.run
            end
            if animate:FindFirstChild("jump") and animate.jump:FindFirstChild("JumpAnim") then
                animate.jump.JumpAnim.AnimationId = "rbxassetid://" .. pack.jump
            end
            if animate:FindFirstChild("fall") and animate.fall:FindFirstChild("FallAnim") then
                animate.fall.FallAnim.AnimationId = "rbxassetid://" .. pack.fall
            end
            if animate:FindFirstChild("climb") and animate.climb:FindFirstChild("ClimbAnim") then
                animate.climb.ClimbAnim.AnimationId = "rbxassetid://" .. pack.climb
            end
            animate.Disabled = false
            hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running)
        end)
    end
    Ony.ApplyAnimationPack = ApplyAnimationPack

    -- // 7.8 Player Target Resolver & SkidFling / HeadSit / Attach / Orbit / Spin / Stare
    local function ResolveSelectedTargetPlayer()
        local selection = tostring(Values["target_player_select"] or "Murderer (Auto)")
        if selection == "Murderer (Auto)" then
            return GetMurdererPlayer() or GetClosestAlivePlayerToPoint(LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position or Vector3.zero)
        elseif selection == "Sheriff (Auto)" then
            return GetSheriffPlayer() or GetMurdererPlayer()
        elseif selection == "Closest Player" then
            local hrp = LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if hrp then
                local p = GetClosestAlivePlayerToPoint(hrp.Position)
                return p
            end
        else
            for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                if plr ~= LocalPlayer and (plr.Name == selection or string.find(selection, plr.Name, 1, true)) then
                    return plr
                end
            end
        end
        return GetMurdererPlayer() or GetSheriffPlayer()
    end
    Ony.ResolveSelectedTargetPlayer = ResolveSelectedTargetPlayer

    local IsExecutingFling = false

    local function ExecuteVelocityFlingOnTarget(targetPlayer, durationSec)
        if IsExecutingFling or not targetPlayer or not targetPlayer.Character then
            return
        end
        local char = LocalPlayer.Character
        local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local tHrp = targetPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not hrp or not tHrp then
            return
        end

        IsExecutingFling = true
        local savedCf = hrp.CFrame
        local startT = os.clock()
        local runTime = durationSec or 0.48

        while (os.clock() - startT < runTime) and hrp.Parent and tHrp.Parent do
            local pred = tHrp.Position + (tHrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity * 0.08)
            hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(pred) * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(math.random(-180, 180)), math.rad(math.random(-180, 180)), 0)
            hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(9000, 9000, 9000)
            hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.new(9000, 9000, 9000)
            RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
        end

        if hrp and hrp.Parent then
            hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
            hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
            hrp.CFrame = savedCf
        end
        IsExecutingFling = false
    end
    Ony.ExecuteVelocityFlingOnTarget = ExecuteVelocityFlingOnTarget

    -- // 7.9 Role Chat Exposer, Discord Webhooks, Smart ServerHop V3 & Config Manager
    Ony.SendChatMessage = function(textMessage)
        pcall(function()
            if TextChatService.ChatVersion == Enum.ChatVersion.TextChatService then
                local channels = TextChatService:FindFirstChild("TextChannels")
                local gen = channels and (channels:FindFirstChild("RBXGeneral") or channels:FindFirstChildOfClass("TextChannel"))
                if gen then
                    gen:SendAsync(tostring(textMessage))
                    return
                end
            end
            local legacyEvents = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("DefaultChatSystemChatEvents")
            local sayReq = legacyEvents and legacyEvents:FindFirstChild("SayMessageRequest")
            if sayReq and sayReq:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
                sayReq:FireServer(tostring(textMessage), "All")
            end
        end)
    end

    Ony.SendWebhookEvent = function(titleText, descriptionText)
        if not ExecutorCaps.HasHttpRequest then
            return
        end
        local webhookUrl = tostring(Ony.GlobalEnv.OnyWtfWebhookUrl or "")
        if webhookUrl == "" or not string.find(webhookUrl, "discord", 1, true) then
            return
        end
        task.spawn(function()
            pcall(function()
                local payload = HttpService:JSONEncode({
                    username = "Ony.wtf MM2 Telemetry",
                    embeds = {
                        {
                            title = tostring(titleText),
                            description = tostring(descriptionText),
                            color = 16726938,
                            footer = {
                                text = "Ony.wtf v5.0.0-MM2 • PlaceId " .. tostring(game.PlaceId),
                            },
                        },
                    },
                })
                Ony.SafeHttpRequest({
                    Url = webhookUrl,
                    Method = "POST",
                    Headers = { ["Content-Type"] = "application/json" },
                    Body = payload,
                })
            end)
        end)
    end

    local function SaveConfigurationToDisk(notify, customPath)
        local targetPath = customPath or CONFIG_FILE
        Ony.GlobalEnv.OnyWtfSavedSessionConfig = {
            Flags = {},
            Values = {},
        }
        for k, v in pairs(Flags) do
            Ony.GlobalEnv.OnyWtfSavedSessionConfig.Flags[k] = v
        end
        for k, v in pairs(Values) do
            Ony.GlobalEnv.OnyWtfSavedSessionConfig.Values[k] = v
        end

        if not ExecutorCaps.HasFileSystem then
            if notify then
                NotifyToast("Config Manager", "Saved custom profile to session memory!")
            end
            return
        end
        pcall(function()
            if not isfolder(CONFIG_FOLDER) then
                makefolder(CONFIG_FOLDER)
            end
            local exportData = {
                Flags = Flags,
                Values = Values,
            }
            writefile(targetPath, HttpService:JSONEncode(exportData))
        end)
        if notify then
            NotifyToast("Config Manager", "Saved configuration to workspace/" .. targetPath .. "!")
        end
    end
    Ony.SaveConfigurationToDisk = SaveConfigurationToDisk

    local function LoadConfigurationFromDisk(notify, customPath)
        local targetPath = customPath or CONFIG_FILE
        if not ExecutorCaps.HasFileSystem then
            local mem = Ony.GlobalEnv.OnyWtfSavedSessionConfig
            if type(mem) == "table" then
                if type(mem.Flags) == "table" then
                    for k, v in pairs(mem.Flags) do
                        Ony.SetFlagState(k, v == true, true)
                    end
                end
                if type(mem.Values) == "table" then
                    for k, v in pairs(mem.Values) do
                        Values[k] = v
                        if Ony.SubValueVisualSyncCallbacks[k] then
                            pcall(Ony.SubValueVisualSyncCallbacks[k], v)
                        end
                    end
                end
                if Ony.UpdateKeybindFlagsHUD then Ony.UpdateKeybindFlagsHUD() end
                if notify then
                    NotifyToast("Config Manager", "Loaded custom profile from session memory!")
                end
            end
            return
        end
        pcall(function()
            if isfile(targetPath) then
                local raw = readfile(targetPath)
                local decoded = HttpService:JSONDecode(raw)
                if type(decoded) == "table" then
                    if type(decoded.Flags) == "table" then
                        for k, v in pairs(decoded.Flags) do
                            Ony.SetFlagState(k, v == true, true)
                        end
                    end
                    if type(decoded.Values) == "table" then
                        for k, v in pairs(decoded.Values) do
                            Values[k] = v
                            if Ony.SubValueVisualSyncCallbacks[k] then
                                pcall(Ony.SubValueVisualSyncCallbacks[k], v)
                            end
                        end
                    end
                    if Ony.UpdateKeybindFlagsHUD then Ony.UpdateKeybindFlagsHUD() end
                    if notify then
                        NotifyToast("Config Manager", "Loaded saved profile from " .. targetPath .. "!")
                    end
                end
            elseif notify then
                NotifyToast("Config Manager", "No saved config file found at " .. targetPath .. "!")
            end
        end)
    end
    Ony.LoadConfigurationFromDisk = LoadConfigurationFromDisk

    local function ApplyPresetConfiguration(presetKey)
        local key = string.lower(tostring(presetKey or "reset"))
        -- 1. First reset all checkboxes to false cleanly
        for k, _ in pairs(Flags) do
            if k == "invisibility" and InvisCloneActive then
                DisableInvisibilityCloneSeamless(false)
            end
            Ony.SetFlagState(k, false, true)
        end

        if key == "reset" or not PRESET_PROFILES[key] then
            if Ony.UpdateKeybindFlagsHUD then Ony.UpdateKeybindFlagsHUD() end
            if Ony.SyncPresetHeaderButtons then pcall(Ony.SyncPresetHeaderButtons, "reset") end
            NotifyToast("Ony.wtf Presets", "All features reset to OFF!")
            return
        end

        local preset = PRESET_PROFILES[key]
        for _, featId in ipairs(preset.Features) do
            Ony.SetFlagState(featId, true, true)
        end
        if type(preset.Values) == "table" then
            for sKey, sVal in pairs(preset.Values) do
                Values[sKey] = sVal
                if Ony.SubValueVisualSyncCallbacks[sKey] then
                    pcall(Ony.SubValueVisualSyncCallbacks[sKey], sVal)
                end
            end
        end

        if Ony.UpdateKeybindFlagsHUD then Ony.UpdateKeybindFlagsHUD() end
        if Ony.SyncPresetHeaderButtons then pcall(Ony.SyncPresetHeaderButtons, key) end
        if Flags["autosave_config"] then
            SaveConfigurationToDisk(false)
        end
        NotifyToast("Ony.wtf Presets", "Loaded [" .. preset.Name .. "] Preset (" .. tostring(#preset.Features) .. " features enabled)!")
    end
    Ony.ApplyPresetConfiguration = ApplyPresetConfiguration

    -- // Auto Re-Execute on Teleport / ServerHop Engine (queue_on_teleport)
    local TeleportQueueRegistered = false

    local function QueueAutoReExecute()
        if TeleportQueueRegistered then
            return
        end
        TeleportQueueRegistered = true

        -- Save active session state so it reloads automatically in the new server
        pcall(function()
            SaveConfigurationToDisk(false, AUTOLOAD_TELEPORT_FILE)
        end)

        pcall(function()
            local queueFn = (type(queue_on_teleport) == "function" and queue_on_teleport)
                or (type(queueonteleport) == "function" and queueonteleport)
                or (type(syn) == "table" and type(syn.queue_on_teleport) == "function" and syn.queue_on_teleport)
                or (type(fluxus) == "table" and type(fluxus.queue_on_teleport) == "function" and fluxus.queue_on_teleport)

            if queueFn then
                local payload = [[
                    repeat task.wait(0.1) until game:IsLoaded()
                    local g = (type(getgenv) == "function" and getgenv()) or _G
                    g.OnyWtfTeleportAutoLoad = true
                    if type(isfile) == "function" and isfile("OnyWtf/Ony.wtf_MM2.luau") then
                        loadstring(readfile("OnyWtf/Ony.wtf_MM2.luau"))()
                    elseif g.OnyWtfScriptSource and type(g.OnyWtfScriptSource) == "string" then
                        loadstring(g.OnyWtfScriptSource)()
                    elseif g.OnyWtfScriptUrl and type(g.OnyWtfScriptUrl) == "string" then
                        loadstring(game:HttpGet(g.OnyWtfScriptUrl))()
                    end
                ]]
                queueFn(payload)
            end
        end)
    end
    Ony.QueueAutoReExecute = QueueAutoReExecute

    -- Hook LocalPlayer.OnTeleport so any server switch while auto_reexecute_teleport is ON queues re-execution
    pcall(function()
        if LocalPlayer and LocalPlayer.OnTeleport then
            Ony.TrackConnection(LocalPlayer.OnTeleport:Connect(function()
                if Flags["auto_reexecute_teleport"] then
                    QueueAutoReExecute()
                end
            end))
        end
    end)

    -- // Smart ServerHop V3 Engine
    local VisitedServerJobIds = { [tostring(game.JobId)] = true }
    pcall(function()
        if ExecutorCaps.HasFileSystem and isfile(VISITED_JOBS_FILE) then
            local decoded = HttpService:JSONDecode(readfile(VISITED_JOBS_FILE))
            if type(decoded) == "table" then
                for k, v in pairs(decoded) do
                    VisitedServerJobIds[tostring(k)] = v
                end
            end
        end
    end)

    local LastServerHopAttemptClock = 0

    local function ExecuteSmartServerHop()
        if os.clock() - LastServerHopAttemptClock < 5 then
            return
        end
        LastServerHopAttemptClock = os.clock()
        QueueAutoReExecute()
        NotifyToast("Smart ServerHop V3", "Queued Auto-ReExecute & scanning populated MM2 servers...")

        task.spawn(function()
            local url = "https://games.roblox.com/v1/games/" .. tostring(game.PlaceId) .. "/servers/Public?sortOrder=Desc&limit=100"
            local res = Ony.SafeHttpRequest({ Url = url, Method = "GET" })
            if res and res.Body then
                local ok, data = pcall(function()
                    return HttpService:JSONDecode(res.Body)
                end)
                if ok and type(data) == "table" and type(data.data) == "table" then
                    local avoidVisited = (Flags["avoid_visited_jobids"] == true)
                    for _, srv in ipairs(data.data) do
                        local idStr = tostring(srv.id or "")
                        local playing = tonumber(srv.playing) or 0
                        local maxPlrs = tonumber(srv.maxPlayers) or 12
                        if idStr ~= "" and idStr ~= tostring(game.JobId) and playing >= 4 and playing < maxPlrs then
                            if (not avoidVisited) or (not VisitedServerJobIds[idStr]) then
                                VisitedServerJobIds[idStr] = true
                                pcall(function()
                                    if ExecutorCaps.HasFileSystem then
                                        if not isfolder(CONFIG_FOLDER) then makefolder(CONFIG_FOLDER) end
                                        writefile(VISITED_JOBS_FILE, HttpService:JSONEncode(VisitedServerJobIds))
                                    end
                                end)
                                NotifyToast("Smart ServerHop V3", "Teleporting to server (" .. tostring(playing) .. "/" .. tostring(maxPlrs) .. ")...")
                                pcall(function()
                                    TeleportService:TeleportToPlaceInstance(game.PlaceId, idStr, LocalPlayer)
                                end)
                                return
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
            pcall(function()
                TeleportService:Teleport(game.PlaceId, LocalPlayer)
            end)
        end)
    end
    Ony.ExecuteSmartServerHop = ExecuteSmartServerHop

    -- // Master Button Action Dispatcher (Handles Every Single Button in MM2_TABS)
    local function ExecuteButtonAction(itemId, defaultToast)
        local char = LocalPlayer.Character
        local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

        if itemId == "shoot_murderer_btn" then
            ShootMurdererWithGun(false, false)
            return
        elseif itemId == "sheriff_insta_win" then
            ShootMurdererWithGun(false, true)
            return
        elseif itemId == "kill_sheriff_only_btn" then
            ExecuteKnifeCombat("Sheriff")
        elseif itemId == "kill_innocents_only_btn" then
            ExecuteKnifeCombat("Innocent")
        elseif itemId == "open_crate_once_btn" then
            LocateMM2Remotes()
            pcall(function()
                local crateName = tostring(Values["selected_crate"] or "Mystery Box 1"):gsub(" ", "")
                if Ony.BoxControllerRemote then
                    if Ony.BoxControllerRemote:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
                        Ony.BoxControllerRemote:FireServer(crateName)
                    elseif Ony.BoxControllerRemote:IsA("BindableEvent") then
                        Ony.BoxControllerRemote:Fire(crateName)
                    end
                end
                local shopRemotes = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Remotes")
                local shopFolder = shopRemotes and shopRemotes:FindFirstChild("Shop")
                local openBox = shopFolder and shopFolder:FindFirstChild("OpenCrate")
                if openBox and openBox:IsA("RemoteFunction") then
                    openBox:InvokeServer(crateName)
                end
            end)
        elseif itemId == "manual_grab_gun_btn" then
            GrabDroppedGun(true)
            return
        elseif itemId == "tp_to_murderer_btn" and hrp then
            local m = GetMurdererPlayer()
            if m and m.Character and m.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                hrp.CFrame = m.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame
            else
                NotifyToast("Teleport", "Murderer not found in active round!")
                return
            end
        elseif itemId == "tp_behind_murderer_btn" and hrp then
            local m = GetMurdererPlayer()
            if m and m.Character and m.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                hrp.CFrame = m.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, 4.5)
            else
                NotifyToast("Teleport", "Murderer not found in active round!")
                return
            end
        elseif itemId == "tp_to_sheriff_btn" and hrp then
            local s = GetSheriffPlayer()
            if s and s.Character and s.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                hrp.CFrame = s.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame
            else
                NotifyToast("Teleport", "Sheriff not found in active round!")
                return
            end
        elseif itemId == "tp_behind_sheriff_btn" and hrp then
            local s = GetSheriffPlayer()
            if s and s.Character and s.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                hrp.CFrame = s.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, 4.5)
            else
                NotifyToast("Teleport", "Sheriff not found in active round!")
                return
            end
        elseif itemId == "tp_dropped_gun_btn" and hrp then
            local gd = Workspace:FindFirstChild("GunDrop", true)
            if gd and gd:IsA("BasePart") then
                hrp.CFrame = gd.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)
            else
                NotifyToast("Teleport", "GunDrop is not currently on the ground!")
                return
            end
        elseif itemId == "tp_safezone_btn" then
            TeleportToSafezonePlatform()
        elseif itemId == "tp_map_spawn_btn" and hrp then
            local map = FindActiveMap()
            local sp = map and map:FindFirstChild("Spawns") and map.Spawns:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")
            if sp then
                hrp.CFrame = sp.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 4, 0)
            else
                NotifyToast("Teleport", "No active round map spawn found!")
                return
            end
        elseif itemId == "tp_lobby_btn" and hrp then
            local sp = Workspace:FindFirstChild("SpawnLocation", true)
            if sp and sp:IsA("BasePart") then
                hrp.CFrame = sp.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 4, 0)
            else
                hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(-108, 138, 18)
            end
        elseif itemId == "tp_high_sky_btn" and hrp then
            hrp.CFrame = hrp.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 60, 0)
        elseif itemId == "fling_all_btn" then
            task.spawn(function()
                for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                    if plr ~= LocalPlayer and IsPlayerInActiveRound(plr) then
                        ExecuteVelocityFlingOnTarget(plr, 0.36)
                        task.wait(0.08)
                    end
                end
            end)
        elseif itemId == "expose_roles_btn" then
            RefreshMM2Roles()
            local msg = string.format(
                "[Ony.wtf] Murderer: %s | Sheriff: %s",
                tostring(Telemetry.MurdererName),
                tostring(Telemetry.SheriffName)
            )
            Ony.SendChatMessage(msg)
            NotifyToast("Role Exposer", msg)
            return
        elseif R15_ANIM_PACKS[itemId] then
            ApplyAnimationPack(itemId)
        elseif itemId == "serverhop_now_btn" then
            ExecuteSmartServerHop()
            return
        elseif itemId == "rejoin_server_btn" then
            QueueAutoReExecute()
            NotifyToast("Rejoin Server", "Queued Auto-ReExecute & rejoining current MM2 server...")
            pcall(function()
                TeleportService:TeleportToPlaceInstance(game.PlaceId, game.JobId, LocalPlayer)
            end)
            return
        elseif itemId == "load_preset_farming_btn" then
            ApplyPresetConfiguration("farming")
            return
        elseif itemId == "load_preset_pvp_btn" then
            ApplyPresetConfiguration("pvp")
            return
        elseif itemId == "load_preset_utility_btn" then
            ApplyPresetConfiguration("utility")
            return
        elseif itemId == "reset_all_config_btn" then
            ApplyPresetConfiguration("reset")
            return
        elseif itemId == "save_config_btn" then
            SaveConfigurationToDisk(true)
            return
        elseif itemId == "load_saved_config_btn" then
            LoadConfigurationFromDisk(true)
            return
        end

        if defaultToast then
            NotifyToast("Ony.wtf MM2", defaultToast)
        end
    end
    Ony.ExecuteButtonAction = ExecuteButtonAction
end

    MainWindowStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(218, 212, 228)
    MainWindowStroke.Thickness = 1.2

    local WindowScale = Instance.new("UIScale", MainWindow)
    local TargetViewportScale = 1
    local function ComputeTargetViewportScale()
        if not Camera then
            return 1
        end
        local vp = Camera.ViewportSize
        if vp.Y < 530 or vp.X < 760 then
            local sx = (vp.X - 32) / 720
            local sy = (vp.Y - 32) / 470
            return MathEngine.Clamp(math.min(sx, sy), 0.55, 1)
        end
        return 1
    end
    local function UpdateViewportScale()
        TargetViewportScale = ComputeTargetViewportScale()
        WindowScale.Scale = TargetViewportScale
    end
    UpdateViewportScale()
    if Camera then
        TrackConnection(Camera:GetPropertyChangedSignal("ViewportSize"):Connect(UpdateViewportScale))
    end

    local MenuAnimating = false
    local function OpenMenuSmooth()
        if MenuAnimating then
            return
        end
        MenuAnimating = true
        TargetViewportScale = ComputeTargetViewportScale()
        FloatingToggleBtn.Visible = false
        MainWindow.Visible = true
        WindowScale.Scale = TargetViewportScale * 0.78
        pcall(function()
            TweenService:Create(
                WindowScale,
                TweenInfo.new(0.28, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
                { Scale = TargetViewportScale }
            ):Play()
        end)
        task.delay(0.29, function()
            WindowScale.Scale = TargetViewportScale
            MenuAnimating = false
        end)
    end

    local function MinimizeMenuSmooth()
        if MenuAnimating then
            return
        end
        MenuAnimating = true
        TargetViewportScale = ComputeTargetViewportScale()
        pcall(function()
            TweenService:Create(
                WindowScale,
                TweenInfo.new(0.18, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.In),
                { Scale = TargetViewportScale * 0.68 }
            ):Play()
        end)
        task.delay(0.18, function()
            MainWindow.Visible = false
            WindowScale.Scale = TargetViewportScale
            FloatingToggleBtn.Visible = true
            FloatScale.Scale = 0.35
            pcall(function()
                TweenService:Create(
                    FloatScale,
                    TweenInfo.new(0.26, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
                    { Scale = 1 }
                ):Play()
            end)
            task.delay(0.26, function()
                FloatScale.Scale = 1
                MenuAnimating = false
            end)
        end)
    end
    Ony.OpenMenuSmooth = OpenMenuSmooth
    Ony.MinimizeMenuSmooth = MinimizeMenuSmooth

    -- // Quick Preset & Config Switcher Bar in Header (Farming | PvP | Utility | Save | Load | Reset)
    local PresetBar = Instance.new("Frame")
    PresetBar.Name = "QuickPresetBar"
    PresetBar.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(338, 24)
    PresetBar.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(146, 10)
    PresetBar.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    PresetBar.ZIndex = 16
    PresetBar.Parent = HeaderBar

    local PresetBarLayout = Instance.new("UIListLayout", PresetBar)
    PresetBarLayout.FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal
    PresetBarLayout.HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Left
    PresetBarLayout.VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Center
    PresetBarLayout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 4)

    local PresetButtons = {}
    local presetDefs = {
        { id = "farming", label = "Farming", width = 62, isPreset = true },
        { id = "pvp", label = "PvP", width = 44, isPreset = true },
        { id = "utility", label = "Utility", width = 52, isPreset = true },
        { id = "save", label = "Save", width = 44, isPreset = false },
        { id = "load", label = "Load", width = 44, isPreset = false },
        { id = "reset", label = "Reset", width = 46, isPreset = false },
    }

    local function UpdatePresetHighlight(activePresetId)
        for pId, btn in pairs(PresetButtons) do
            local isAct = (pId == activePresetId)
            btn.BackgroundColor3 = isAct and Ony.AccentColor or Color3.fromRGB(235, 232, 241)
            btn.TextColor3 = isAct and Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255) or Color3.fromRGB(55, 48, 68)
        end
    end
    Ony.UpdatePresetHighlight = UpdatePresetHighlight

    for _, pDef in ipairs(presetDefs) do
        local pBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
        pBtn.Name = "Preset_" .. pDef.id
        pBtn.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(pDef.width, 22)
        pBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(235, 232, 241)
        pBtn.Text = pDef.label
        pBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        pBtn.TextSize = 10
        pBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(55, 48, 68)
        pBtn.ClipsDescendants = true
        pBtn.ZIndex = 17
        pBtn.Parent = PresetBar
        Instance.new("UICorner", pBtn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 5)
        local pStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", pBtn)
        pStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(214, 208, 225)
        pStroke.Thickness = 1

        if pDef.isPreset then
            PresetButtons[pDef.id] = pBtn
        end

        TrackConnection(pBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            if pDef.isPreset then
                if ApplyPresetConfiguration then
                    ApplyPresetConfiguration(pDef.id)
                end
                UpdatePresetHighlight(pDef.id)
            elseif pDef.id == "save" then
                SaveConfigurationToDisk(false)
            elseif pDef.id == "load" then
                LoadConfigurationFromDisk(false)
                UpdatePresetHighlight(nil)
            elseif pDef.id == "reset" then
                ExecuteButtonAction("reset_all_config_btn", "All features reset to OFF.")
                UpdatePresetHighlight(nil)
            end
        end))
    end

    end))

    -- // Draggable Animated Vector Logo Button + Instant Weight Squish on Click -> Smooth Menu Open
    do
        local orbDragging = false
        local orbMoved = false
        local orbDragInput, orbDragStart, orbStartPos

        TrackConnection(FloatingToggleBtn.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
            if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                orbDragging = true
                orbMoved = false
                orbDragStart = input.Position
                orbStartPos = FloatingToggleBtn.Position
                pcall(function()
                    TweenService:Create(
                        FloatScale,
                        TweenInfo.new(0.08, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
                        { Scale = 0.88 }
                    ):Play()
                end)
                input.Changed:Connect(function()
                    if input.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then
                        orbDragging = false
                        if not orbMoved then
                            -- Instant Weight Compression ("Anlık Ağırlık Küçülmesi") Before Smooth Menu Open
                            FloatScale.Scale = 0.72
                            pcall(function()
                                TweenService:Create(
                                    FloatScale,
                                    TweenInfo.new(0.09, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
                                    { Scale = 0.68 }
                                ):Play()
                            end)
                            task.delay(0.09, function()
                                OpenMenuSmooth()
                            end)
                        else
                            pcall(function()
                                TweenService:Create(
                                    FloatScale,
                                    TweenInfo.new(0.16, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
                                    { Scale = 1 }
                                ):Play()
                            end)
                        end
                    end
                end)
            end
        end))

        TrackConnection(FloatingToggleBtn.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
            if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                orbDragInput = input
            end
        end))

        TrackConnection(UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
            if input == orbDragInput and orbDragging and orbDragStart and orbStartPos then
                local delta = input.Position - orbDragStart
                if delta.Magnitude > 5 then
                    orbMoved = true
                    FloatingToggleBtn.Position = UDim2.new(
                        orbStartPos.X.Scale,
                        orbStartPos.X.Offset + delta.X,
                        orbStartPos.Y.Scale,
                        orbStartPos.Y.Offset + delta.Y
                    )
                end
            end
        end))
    end

    -- // Smooth Dragging of MainWindow on PC Mouse & Mobile Touch
    do
        local dragging = false
        local dragInput, dragStart, startPos

        TrackConnection(HeaderBar.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
            if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                dragging = true
                dragStart = input.Position
                startPos = MainWindow.Position
                input.Changed:Connect(function()
                    if input.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then
                        dragging = false
                    end
                end)
            end
        end))

        TrackConnection(HeaderBar.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
            if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                dragInput = input
            end
        end))

        TrackConnection(UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
            if input == dragInput and dragging and dragStart and startPos then
                local delta = input.Position - dragStart
                MainWindow.Position = UDim2.new(
                    startPos.X.Scale,
                    startPos.X.Offset + delta.X,
                    startPos.Y.Scale,
                    startPos.Y.Offset + delta.Y
                )
            end
        end))
    end

    rgbInfoLbl.Text = "R:255 G:59 B:154"
    rgbInfoLbl.Font = Enum.Font.Code

    rgbInfoLbl.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(205, 210, 220)

        Color3.fromRGB(255, 59, 154),

        Color3.fromRGB(254, 231, 92),
