    local dpState = {
        h = 0.91,
        s = 0.77,
        v = 1.0,
        origColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 59, 154),
        targetItemId = nil,
        targetSwatchIdx = 1,
        targetButton = nil,
    }

    local function Color3ToHex(col)
        local r = math.clamp(math.floor(col.R * 255 + 0.5), 0, 255)
        local g = math.clamp(math.floor(col.G * 255 + 0.5), 0, 255)
        local b = math.clamp(math.floor(col.B * 255 + 0.5), 0, 255)
        return string.format("#%02X%02X%02X", r, g, b), r, g, b
    end

    local function SyncDiscordPickerVisuals()
        local col = Color3.fromHSV(dpState.h, dpState.s, dpState.v)
        svCanvas.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromHSV(dpState.h, 1, 1)
        svCursor.Position = UDim2.fromScale(dpState.s, 1 - dpState.v)
        svCursor.BackgroundColor3 = col
        hueThumb.Position = UDim2.fromScale(dpState.h, 0.5)
        dpPreviewBox.BackgroundColor3 = col
        local hx, r, g, b = Color3ToHex(col)
        hexBox.Text = hx
        rgbInfoLbl.Text = string.format("R:%d G:%d B:%d", r, g, b)
        if dpState.targetButton then
            dpState.targetButton.BackgroundColor3 = col
        end
        if dpState.targetItemId and ItemColors[dpState.targetItemId] then
            ItemColors[dpState.targetItemId][dpState.targetSwatchIdx] = col
        end
    end

    OpenDiscordColorPicker = function(itemId, itemLabel, swatchIdx, swatchBtn)
        local curCol = (ItemColors[itemId] and ItemColors[itemId][swatchIdx]) or swatchBtn.BackgroundColor3 or Ony.AccentColor
        local h, s, v = Color3.toHSV(curCol)
        dpState.h = h
        dpState.s = s
        dpState.v = v
        dpState.origColor = curCol
        dpState.targetItemId = itemId
        dpState.targetSwatchIdx = swatchIdx
        dpState.targetButton = swatchBtn
        dpTitle.Text = "Discord Color Picker | " .. tostring(itemLabel) .. " (#" .. tostring(swatchIdx) .. ")"
        SyncDiscordPickerVisuals()
        DiscordPickerOverlay.Visible = true
    end
    Ony.OpenDiscordColorPicker = OpenDiscordColorPicker

    for _, presetCol in ipairs(DISCORD_SWATCHES) do
        local psBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
        psBtn.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(26, 22)
        psBtn.BackgroundColor3 = presetCol
        psBtn.Text = ""
        psBtn.ZIndex = 84
        psBtn.Parent = dpPresetsHolder
        Instance.new("UICorner", psBtn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 4)
        TrackConnection(psBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            local h, s, v = Color3.toHSV(presetCol)
            dpState.h, dpState.s, dpState.v = h, s, v
            SyncDiscordPickerVisuals()
        end))
    end

    local draggingSV = false
    local draggingHue = false
    local function UpdateSVFromScreen(pos)
        local rx = MathEngine.Clamp((pos.X - svCanvas.AbsolutePosition.X) / math.max(1, svCanvas.AbsoluteSize.X), 0, 1)
        local ry = MathEngine.Clamp((pos.Y - svCanvas.AbsolutePosition.Y) / math.max(1, svCanvas.AbsoluteSize.Y), 0, 1)
        dpState.s = rx
        dpState.v = 1 - ry
        SyncDiscordPickerVisuals()
    end
    local function UpdateHueFromScreen(pos)
        local rx = MathEngine.Clamp((pos.X - hueTrack.AbsolutePosition.X) / math.max(1, hueTrack.AbsoluteSize.X), 0, 1)
        dpState.h = rx
        SyncDiscordPickerVisuals()
    end

    TrackConnection(svCanvas.InputBegan:Connect(function(inp)
        if inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            draggingSV = true
            UpdateSVFromScreen(inp.Position)
        end
    end))
    TrackConnection(hueTrack.InputBegan:Connect(function(inp)
        if inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            draggingHue = true
            UpdateHueFromScreen(inp.Position)
        end
    end))
    TrackConnection(UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(inp)
        if inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            if draggingSV then
                UpdateSVFromScreen(inp.Position)
            elseif draggingHue then
                UpdateHueFromScreen(inp.Position)
            end
        end
    end))
    TrackConnection(UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(inp)
        if inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            draggingSV = false
            draggingHue = false
        end
    end))
    TrackConnection(hexBox.FocusLost:Connect(function()
        local cleaned = string.gsub(hexBox.Text or "", "[^0-9a-fA-F]", "")
        if #cleaned == 6 then
            local r = tonumber(string.sub(cleaned, 1, 2), 16) or 255
            local g = tonumber(string.sub(cleaned, 3, 4), 16) or 59
            local b = tonumber(string.sub(cleaned, 5, 6), 16) or 154
            local h, s, v = Color3.toHSV(Color3.fromRGB(r, g, b))
            dpState.h, dpState.s, dpState.v = h, s, v
            SyncDiscordPickerVisuals()
        end
    end))
    TrackConnection(dpResetBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        local h, s, v = Color3.toHSV(dpState.origColor)
        dpState.h, dpState.s, dpState.v = h, s, v
        SyncDiscordPickerVisuals()
    end))
    TrackConnection(dpApplyBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        DiscordPickerOverlay.Visible = false
    end))
    TrackConnection(dpCloseBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        DiscordPickerOverlay.Visible = false
    end))
    end

    local TabButtons = {}
    local TabIndicators = {}
    local TabPages = {}
    local AllGroupFrames = {}
    local AllGroupCardsThemeList = {}
    local AllLabelsThemeList = {}
    local LayoutRefreshCallbacks = {}
    local ActiveModuleMode = "hub_universal" -- "hub_universal" (Dark Mode) or "mm2"
    Ony.AllGroupFrames = AllGroupFrames

    local function RefreshAllLayouts()
        for _, fn in ipairs(LayoutRefreshCallbacks) do
            pcall(fn)
        end
    end

    local function SelectTab(tabId)
        local isDarkTheme = (ActiveModuleMode == "hub_universal")
        for id, page in pairs(TabPages) do
            local isSelected = (id == tabId)
            page.Visible = isSelected
            local btn = TabButtons[id]
            local ind = TabIndicators[id]
            if btn then
                btn.TextColor3 = isSelected and Ony.AccentColor or (isDarkTheme and Color3.fromRGB(235, 230, 245) or Color3.fromRGB(45, 40, 54))
                btn.BackgroundTransparency = isSelected and 0.88 or 1
            end
            if ind then
                ind.Visible = isSelected
            end
        end
        RefreshAllLayouts()
    end

    -- // Build All Feature Tabs (Both MM2 & Universal Tabs from UI_SCHEMA)
    for _, tabDef in ipairs(Ony.UI_SCHEMA) do
        local tBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")

        tBtn.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(112, 28)
        tBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Ony.AccentColor
        tBtn.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        tBtn.Text = "   " .. tabDef.label
        tBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        tBtn.TextSize = 11
        tBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(45, 40, 54)

        local leftCol = Instance.new("Frame")

        local rightCol = Instance.new("Frame")

        rightCol.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 6, 0, 0)
        rightCol.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        rightCol.ZIndex = 14

        local rightLayout = Instance.new("UIListLayout", rightCol)
        rightLayout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
        rightLayout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 10)

        local function SyncPageCanvas()
            local lh = leftLayout.AbsoluteContentSize.Y
            local rh = rightLayout.AbsoluteContentSize.Y
            leftCol.Size = UDim2.new(0.5, -6, 0, lh)
            rightCol.Size = UDim2.new(0.5, -6, 0, rh)
            page.CanvasSize = UDim2.fromOffset(0, math.max(lh, rh) + 32)
        end
        TrackConnection(leftLayout:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteContentSize"):Connect(SyncPageCanvas))
        TrackConnection(rightLayout:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteContentSize"):Connect(SyncPageCanvas))
        table.insert(LayoutRefreshCallbacks, SyncPageCanvas)

        for gIdx, groupDef in ipairs(tabDef.groups) do
            local groupCard = Instance.new("Frame")
            groupCard.Name = "Group_" .. groupDef.id
            groupCard.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 100)
            groupCard.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(239, 237, 243)
            groupCard.BorderSizePixel = 0
            groupCard.LayoutOrder = gIdx

            Instance.new("UICorner", groupCard).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)

            gStroke.Thickness = 1
            table.insert(AllGroupCardsThemeList, { card = groupCard, stroke = gStroke })

            local topLine = Instance.new("Frame")

            topLine.BorderSizePixel = 0
            topLine.BackgroundColor3 = Ony.AccentColor

            topLine.Parent = groupCard

            local itemsContainer = Instance.new("Frame")

            itemsContainer.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 8)
            itemsContainer.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            itemsContainer.ZIndex = 16
            itemsContainer.Parent = groupCard

            local icLayout = Instance.new("UIListLayout", itemsContainer)
            icLayout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
            icLayout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 5)

            local function SyncGroupSize()
                local h = icLayout.AbsoluteContentSize.Y
                itemsContainer.Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, h)
                groupCard.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, h + 18)
                SyncPageCanvas()
            end
            TrackConnection(icLayout:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteContentSize"):Connect(SyncGroupSize))
            table.insert(LayoutRefreshCallbacks, SyncGroupSize)

            local gHeader = Instance.new("TextLabel")
            gHeader.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 16)
            gHeader.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            gHeader.Text = "- " .. groupDef.title .. " -"
            gHeader.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
            gHeader.TextSize = 11
            gHeader.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(45, 36, 54)
            gHeader.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
            gHeader.LayoutOrder = 0
            gHeader.ZIndex = 16
            gHeader.Parent = itemsContainer
            table.insert(AllLabelsThemeList, gHeader)

            if groupDef.id == "autofarm_coins" then
                local statsBox = Instance.new("Frame")
                statsBox.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 38)
                statsBox.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
                statsBox.LayoutOrder = 1
                statsBox.ZIndex = 16
                statsBox.Parent = itemsContainer
                Instance.new("UICorner", statsBox).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 4)

                local FarmStatsLabelLine1 = Instance.new("TextLabel")
                FarmStatsLabelLine1.Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, 16)
                FarmStatsLabelLine1.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(6, 3)
                FarmStatsLabelLine1.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                FarmStatsLabelLine1.Text = "Farm Live Stats: IDLE | Coins: 0 / 50 (0%)"
                FarmStatsLabelLine1.Font = Enum.Font.Code
                FarmStatsLabelLine1.TextSize = 10
                FarmStatsLabelLine1.TextColor3 = Ony.AccentColor
                FarmStatsLabelLine1.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
                FarmStatsLabelLine1.ZIndex = 17
                FarmStatsLabelLine1.Parent = statsBox
                Ony.FarmStatsLabelLine1 = FarmStatsLabelLine1

                local FarmStatsLabelLine2 = Instance.new("TextLabel")
                FarmStatsLabelLine2.Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, 16)
                FarmStatsLabelLine2.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(6, 19)
                FarmStatsLabelLine2.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                FarmStatsLabelLine2.Text = "Target: CoinContainer | Speed: 22 s/s"
                FarmStatsLabelLine2.Font = Enum.Font.Code
                FarmStatsLabelLine2.TextSize = 10
                FarmStatsLabelLine2.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(70, 62, 84)
                FarmStatsLabelLine2.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
                FarmStatsLabelLine2.ZIndex = 17
                FarmStatsLabelLine2.Parent = statsBox
                Ony.FarmStatsLabelLine2 = FarmStatsLabelLine2
            end

            local collectedLabels = { groupDef.title }

            for iIdx, itemDef in ipairs(groupDef.items) do
                table.insert(collectedLabels, itemDef.label)

                local itemWrapper = Instance.new("Frame")
                itemWrapper.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 22)
                itemWrapper.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                itemWrapper.LayoutOrder = iIdx + 2
                itemWrapper.ZIndex = 16
                itemWrapper.Parent = itemsContainer

                local iwLayout = Instance.new("UIListLayout", itemWrapper)
                iwLayout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
                iwLayout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 4)

                local function SyncWrapperSize()
                    itemWrapper.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, iwLayout.AbsoluteContentSize.Y)
                    SyncGroupSize()
                end
                TrackConnection(iwLayout:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteContentSize"):Connect(SyncWrapperSize))
                table.insert(LayoutRefreshCallbacks, SyncWrapperSize)

                if itemDef.type == "button" then
                    local actBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
                    actBtn.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 24)
                    actBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(226, 222, 235)
                    actBtn.Text = "  " .. itemDef.label .. "   >"
                    actBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold
                    actBtn.TextSize = 11
                    actBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(35, 31, 42)
                    actBtn.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
                    actBtn.ZIndex = 17
                    actBtn.Parent = itemWrapper
                    Instance.new("UICorner", actBtn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 4)

                    TrackConnection(actBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                        ExecuteButtonAction(itemDef.id, itemDef.toast)
                    end))
                else
                    local row = Instance.new("Frame")
                    row.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 22)
                    row.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                    row.LayoutOrder = 1
                    row.ZIndex = 17
                    row.Parent = itemWrapper

                    local cbBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
                    cbBtn.Size = UDim2.new(1, -92, 1, 0)
                    cbBtn.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                    cbBtn.Text = ""
                    cbBtn.ZIndex = 18
                    cbBtn.Parent = row

                    local boxFrame = Instance.new("Frame")
                    boxFrame.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(14, 14)
                    boxFrame.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 4)
                    boxFrame.BackgroundColor3 = Flags[itemDef.id] and Ony.AccentColor or Color3.fromRGB(214, 209, 224)
                    boxFrame.ZIndex = 18
                    boxFrame.Parent = cbBtn
                    Instance.new("UICorner", boxFrame).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 3)

                    local checkMark = Instance.new("TextLabel")
                    checkMark.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
                    checkMark.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                    checkMark.Text = Flags[itemDef.id] and "v" or ""
                    checkMark.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
                    checkMark.TextSize = 11
                    checkMark.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
                    checkMark.ZIndex = 19
                    checkMark.Parent = boxFrame

                    local lbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
                    lbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, -22, 1, 0)
                    lbl.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(20, 0)
                    lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                    lbl.Text = itemDef.label
                    lbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
                    lbl.TextSize = 12
                    lbl.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(35, 31, 42)
                    lbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
                    lbl.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
                    lbl.ZIndex = 18
                    lbl.Parent = cbBtn
                    table.insert(AllLabelsThemeList, lbl)

                    local function UpdateCheckboxVisual(on)
                        boxFrame.BackgroundColor3 = on and Ony.AccentColor or Color3.fromRGB(214, 209, 224)
                        checkMark.Text = on and "v" or ""
                    end
                    Ony.FlagVisualSyncCallbacks[itemDef.id] = UpdateCheckboxVisual

                    TrackConnection(cbBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                        local nextVal = not Flags[itemDef.id]
                        SetFlagState(itemDef.id, nextVal, false)
                        UpdateKeybindFlagsHUD()
                        if Flags["autosave_config"] then
                            SaveConfigurationToDisk(false)
                        end
                        NotifyToast("Ony.wtf MM2", itemDef.label .. ": " .. (nextVal and "ENABLED" or "DISABLED"))
                    end))

                    local rightControls = Instance.new("Frame")
                    rightControls.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(90, 22)
                    rightControls.Position = UDim2.new(1, -90, 0, 0)
                    rightControls.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                    rightControls.ZIndex = 18
                    rightControls.Parent = row

                    local rcLayout = Instance.new("UIListLayout", rightControls)
                    rcLayout.FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal
                    rcLayout.HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Right
                    rcLayout.VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Center
                    rcLayout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 4)

                    if itemDef.colors then
                        for cIdx, initCol in ipairs(itemDef.colors) do
                            local swatchBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
                            swatchBtn.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(14, 12)
                            swatchBtn.BackgroundColor3 = initCol
                            swatchBtn.Text = ""
                            swatchBtn.ZIndex = 19
                            swatchBtn.Parent = rightControls
                            Instance.new("UICorner", swatchBtn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 3)

                            TrackConnection(swatchBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                                OpenDiscordColorPicker(itemDef.id, itemDef.label, cIdx, swatchBtn)
                            end))

                    end

                    if itemDef.keybind then
                        local kbBadge = Instance.new("TextLabel")
                        kbBadge.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(24, 16)
                        kbBadge.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(230, 226, 238)
                        kbBadge.Text = "[" .. tostring(itemDef.keybind) .. "]"

                        kbBadge.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(95, 86, 110)
                        kbBadge.ZIndex = 19
                        kbBadge.Parent = rightControls
                        Instance.new("UICorner", kbBadge).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 3)

                    if itemDef.subSettings and #itemDef.subSettings > 0 then

                        gearBtn.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(20, 18)
                        gearBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(228, 224, 236)
                        gearBtn.Text = "+"
                        gearBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
                        gearBtn.TextSize = 12
                        gearBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(85, 78, 98)

                        subDrawer.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 40)
                        subDrawer.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(248, 247, 252)

                        subInner.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(8, 6)

                        sdLayout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 5)

                            local sh = sdLayout.AbsoluteContentSize.Y
                            subInner.Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 0, sh)
                            subDrawer.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, sh + 12)

                            gearBtn.Text = subDrawer.Visible and "-" or "+"
                            gearBtn.TextColor3 = subDrawer.Visible and Ony.AccentColor or Color3.fromRGB(85, 78, 98)
                            SyncSubDrawer()
                        end))

                        for sIdx, subDef in ipairs(itemDef.subSettings) do
                            if subDef.type == "slider" then
                                local sFrame = Instance.new("Frame")
                                sFrame.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 28)
                                sFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                                sFrame.LayoutOrder = sIdx

                                sTop.ZIndex = 19

                                track.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 18)

                                track.Text = ""
                                track.ZIndex = 19
                                track.Parent = sFrame
                                Instance.new("UICorner", track).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)

                                local initPct = MathEngine.Clamp((tonumber(Values[subDef.id]) - subDef.min) / math.max(1, subDef.max - subDef.min), 0, 1)
                                local fill = Instance.new("Frame")
                                fill.Size = UDim2.fromScale(initPct, 1)
                                fill.BackgroundColor3 = Ony.AccentColor
                                fill.BorderSizePixel = 0
                                fill.ZIndex = 20
                                fill.Parent = track
                                Instance.new("UICorner", fill).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)

                                Ony.SubValueVisualSyncCallbacks[subDef.id] = function(newVal)
                                    local pct = MathEngine.Clamp((tonumber(newVal) - subDef.min) / math.max(1, subDef.max - subDef.min), 0, 1)
                                    fill.Size = UDim2.fromScale(pct, 1)
                                    sTop.Text = subDef.label .. ": " .. tostring(newVal) .. (subDef.unit or "")
                                end

                                local function UpdateSliderFromX(screenX)
                                    local rel = MathEngine.Clamp((screenX - track.AbsolutePosition.X) / math.max(1, track.AbsoluteSize.X), 0, 1)
                                    local rawVal = subDef.min + (subDef.max - subDef.min) * rel
                                    local stepped = math.floor(rawVal / subDef.step + 0.5) * subDef.step
                                    Values[subDef.id] = stepped
                                    fill.Size = UDim2.fromScale(rel, 1)
                                    sTop.Text = subDef.label .. ": " .. tostring(stepped) .. (subDef.unit or "")
                                    if Flags["autosave_config"] then
                                        SaveConfigurationToDisk(false)
                                    end
                                end

                                local sliding = false
                                TrackConnection(track.InputBegan:Connect(function(inp)
                                    if inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                                        sliding = true
                                        UpdateSliderFromX(inp.Position.X)
                                    end
                                end))
                                TrackConnection(UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(inp)
                                    if sliding and (inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
                                        UpdateSliderFromX(inp.Position.X)
                                    end
                                end))
                                TrackConnection(UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(inp)
                                    if inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                                        sliding = false
                                    end
                                end))
                            elseif subDef.type == "dropdown" and subDef.options then
                                local ddBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
                                ddBtn.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 20)
                                ddBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(234, 230, 242)
                                ddBtn.Text = "  " .. subDef.label .. ": " .. tostring(Values[subDef.id])
                                ddBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
                                ddBtn.TextSize = 10
                                ddBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(45, 38, 56)
                                ddBtn.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
                                ddBtn.LayoutOrder = sIdx
                                ddBtn.ZIndex = 19
                                ddBtn.Parent = subInner
                                Instance.new("UICorner", ddBtn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 4)

                                Ony.SubValueVisualSyncCallbacks[subDef.id] = function(newVal)
                                    ddBtn.Text = "  " .. subDef.label .. ": " .. tostring(newVal)
                                end

                                local optIdx = 1
                                TrackConnection(ddBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                                    local dynamicOpts = subDef.options
                                    if subDef.id == "target_player_select" then
                                        dynamicOpts = { "Murderer (Auto)", "Sheriff (Auto)", "Closest Player" }
                                        for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                                            if p ~= LocalPlayer then
                                                table.insert(dynamicOpts, p.Name)
                                            end
                                        end
                                    end
                                    optIdx = (optIdx % #dynamicOpts) + 1
                                    local chosen = dynamicOpts[optIdx]
                                    Values[subDef.id] = chosen
                                    ddBtn.Text = "  " .. subDef.label .. ": " .. tostring(chosen)
                                end))
                            elseif subDef.type == "toggle" then
                                local stBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
                                stBtn.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 18)
                                stBtn.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                                stBtn.Text = subDef.label .. ": " .. (Values[subDef.id] and "ON" or "OFF")
                                stBtn.Font = Enum.Font.Code
                                stBtn.TextSize = 10
                                stBtn.TextColor3 = Values[subDef.id] and Ony.AccentColor or Color3.fromRGB(95, 88, 108)
                                stBtn.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
                                stBtn.LayoutOrder = sIdx
                                stBtn.ZIndex = 19
                                stBtn.Parent = subInner

                                Ony.SubValueVisualSyncCallbacks[subDef.id] = function(newVal)
                                    stBtn.Text = subDef.label .. ": " .. (newVal and "ON" or "OFF")
                                    stBtn.TextColor3 = newVal and Ony.AccentColor or Color3.fromRGB(95, 88, 108)
                                end

                                TrackConnection(stBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                                    Values[subDef.id] = not Values[subDef.id]
                                    stBtn.Text = subDef.label .. ": " .. (Values[subDef.id] and "ON" or "OFF")
                                    stBtn.TextColor3 = Values[subDef.id] and Ony.AccentColor or Color3.fromRGB(95, 88, 108)
                                end))
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end

            table.insert(AllGroupFrames, {
                frame = groupCard,
                title = string.lower(groupDef.title),
                itemLabels = collectedLabels,
            })
        end

        TabPages[tabDef.id] = page
        TrackConnection(tBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            SelectTab(tabDef.id)
        end))
    end
